營收走勢圖

研究員/蔡佾璋 EASONTSAI@uni-psg.com

研究部維持南亞(1303)之投資建議為買進，主要考量：(1)南亞 7
月自結稅後 EPS 0.4 元，雖不如 6 月自結稅後 EPS 2.88 元，但因
6 月有包含權益法認列收益不具可比性，而具可比性的 4、5、7 月
自結稅前淨利率分別是 9.4%、13%、15.7%，歸母淨利率為 4.7%、
9.2%、10.4%，獲利顯著持續提升，符合研究部預期。營收方面，
7+8 月營收達成率 70.6%，優於預期，公司展望 9 月營收將優於 8
月，4Q26 的營收將優於 3Q26，整體營運維持正向。(2)應用於 AI
伺服器的 M6-M8 高階大訂單目前正與客戶洽談生產配合及廠區產
線對接等細節，預計將於 4Q26 完成協商並開始有明顯進展，帶動
後續營收與利潤大幅優化，去瓶頸後的 30 萬張 CCL 產能將全數投
入高階產品生產。(3)廠商持續將擴產資源湧入中高階材料，導致
以 PCB 為主軸的玻纖布、銅箔、CCL、ABF 載板等中低階電子材料
依然緊缺，研究部預期南亞電子材料漲價態勢將延續。此外，南亞
積極推動 AI 供應鏈高階材料的開發、認證與銷售，有顯著成效，
2027 年電子相關獲利占比也將提升至 98%，電子材料占比可望達
70-80%，優於研究部原先預估的65-70%。(4)子公司方面，受惠AI
需求旺盛 ， 南電產能有限但需求持續放大 ，ABF 載板漲價效應延續 ，
2026、2027 年業績皆會有明顯提升；南亞科受惠記憶體供需缺口
再擴大，利潤急速成長。(5)近期石化產品 EG 價格大漲，利差顯著
擴大，對獲利明顯幫助，若市況持續好轉，更明顯的獲利貢獻會落
在 4Q26。研究部分別上修南亞 2026 年、2027 年稅後 EPS 至 13.82
元、21.64 元(原估 12.02 元、16.94 元)，分別+2,325%YoY、
+56.6%YoY，年底淨值 69.8 元、91.4 元。研究部認為 2027 年 CSP
業者對整機成本及零組件價格控管趨嚴 ， 加上上游原料新增產能於
2H27 至1H28 陸續開出，電子材料及CCL 業者議價能力會降低，因
此將電子材料目標評價調降至 20 倍，惟考量目前股價隱含電子材
料評價僅約 12 倍，評價極低，故維持南亞買進投資建議。以2027
年電子材料:南亞科:傳產獲利占比 48:50:2，電子與傳產淨值占比
約 4：6 之比例推算，目標價調升至320 元(20xPER*2027 本業電子
材料 EPS + 5xPER*2027 南亞科貢獻稅後 EPS + 1xPBR*2027 傳產
BVPS)。

重點摘要： 
1. 南亞產品線包括在石化、塑膠、纖維與電子類，分佈廣泛

圖 1、南亞 1H26 部門別營收比重 
電子材料
52.7%
化工產品
16.9%
聚酯產品
15.8%
塑膠加工
12.7%
其他
1.9%

資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞是全球最大塑膠加工廠，此外，其電子級玻纖絲為全球
第二大 ， 銅箔基板 、 玻纖布為全球第三大 ， 乙二醇EG為全球第
四大，環氧樹脂、丙二酚BPA為全球第五大業者。南亞產能全
球布局，主要生產基地在台灣，有57個廠， 中國有34個， 美國
有8個廠，共99個生產廠。

同時，南亞持有 26.3%的南亞科為全球第四大 DRAM廠，持有
61.4%的子公司南電為 「PCB三雄」 之一 ， 穩居全球前五大IC載
板廠，也是全球前十大PCB製造商。南亞的產品線為台塑集團
中最廣泛，分佈在石化、 纖維 、塑膠加工及電子材料等，1H26
電子材料佔比由 2025年的46.4% 提升到52.7%，化工產品由
21.8%降至16.9%，聚酯產品由16%略降至15.8%，塑膠產品由
13.9%降至12.7%， 其他1.9%。 公司生產的原材料多為集團內部
自用 ， 僅約少部分對外銷售 ， 外售若對公司更有幫助的話 ， 公
司將會提高外售比率。

聚酯產品包括聚酯薄膜、聚酯纖維(如聚酯棉、POY、DTY以及
瓶用酯粒)等 。 電子材料產品包括銅箔 、 銅箔基板(CCL)、 玻纖
絲、玻纖布、環氧樹脂(EPOXY)等；塑膠產品包括膠布、膠皮
與PU合成皮等。銷售地區廣泛，以中國、美國、台灣為主。

2. 子公司南電、南亞科漲價趨勢延續，母公司南亞間接受惠 
由於AI應用需求大增 ，ASIC需求預估在2026年大幅增加 ， 此類
產品全都用ABF載板 ，ABF載板於4Q25起漲 ，1Q26及2Q26皆維持
續漲價節奏。研究部認為接下來ABF廠議價能力轉強，南電報
價得以持續上漲，母公司南亞間接受惠。

南亞科2Q26持續繳出亮眼的成績，GM及OPM分別達到79.5%及
73.7%的水準 ， 不亞於一線大廠 ， 單季稅後EPS 14.66元 。 南亞
科預期3Q26營運及OPM將進一步提升 ， 且短缺情況至4Q26未見
變化 ， 同時預期市場在2028年前不會發生反轉 ， 母公司南亞間
接受惠。

股權變動方面，董事會之前決議處分南電 3%與南亞科1%的股
權 ， 其中 ， 南電的第一個3%已經處分完畢 ， 後來董事會又通過
再處分3%， 大部分已於2Q26底處分完畢 ， 目前沒有新的處分計
畫，兩次重大處分後仍持有南電61%股權。

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  4

2026/3/25南亞科董事會宣布晟碟 （Sandisk） 、 鎧俠 （Kioxia）、
SK Hynix旗下的Solidigm、思科（Cisco）四家公司分三次私
募認購普通股共3.5億股 ， 股權稀釋約11.3%,研究部推算目前
南亞持有南亞科之持股比例約為26.3%。 而南亞持有南亞科的
3,099萬股權處分目前尚未執行，年底前也不一定會處分，預
估執行後持股比例將調整至25.4%。

3. 電子材料營收自1H26的52.7%提升至9月的60%以上

表1、電子材料為南亞獲利主體(營業利益佔比) 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

缺貨造成電子材料持續漲價，2025年初以來最先出現明顯供
給吃緊的玻纖 ， 近期已擴散至銅箔 、 銅箔基板等材料 ， 各材料
缺貨、漲價還會延續一段時間。中低階產品缺貨主因在於高
階化帶來的產能排擠，隨著玻纖、銅箔及基板高階產品製程
精度提升，生產速度下降，不論銅箔、玻纖、基板，要生產1
單位的高階材料，都必須放棄生產很多單位的中低階材料，
導致缺貨更嚴重，此情況將維持未來一段時間。

電子材料持續成為南亞營運轉型核心，產線維持高稼動率，
銅箔基板、玻纖布與玻纖絲產能利用率達滿載，銅箔與環氧
樹脂產能利用率在8-9成之間。8月電子材料營收占比已提升
至59.8%，較7月增加2.8pct， 正式逼近六成 。 電子材料產業到
2028、2029年仍可維持榮景。

業界有開始擴產，但擴產速度本身有限制，且擴產後也還是
會面臨材料的問題，此外，PCB層數大幅增加，將使得供給增
加，幅度仍跟不上需求的增速 。 整體而言 ， 電子材料供需偏緊
與漲價趨勢短期難以扭轉 ， 銅箔基板及高階PCB的浪潮會有望
延續。

### Page 5

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  5

4. 玻纖獲利率最高，獲利貢獻CCL最大，全品項OPM持續提升 
表 2、南亞各產品別 OPM 逐季轉佳 
資料來源：公司、統一投顧整理

南亞產品價格漲幅因客戶依賴度而異，依客戶每個月機動調
整 。 由於中高階產品供不應求 ， 加上同業將產能轉做高階 ， 導
致一般消費性電子用的E-Glass產能吃緊 ， 這對產品報價相當
有利，報價自2025年開始每個月約調漲5-10%。

獲利率方面，現階段電子材料獲利率以玻纖紗/布排行第一、
近期CCL跟載板漲幅度大，獲利率已逐漸接近玻纖，排名升至
第二，再來是銅箔，而環氧樹脂有8成為非電子用途，因此獲
利率較銅箔低些。

獲利金額貢獻方面，雖然目前獲利率最高的是玻纖紗/布，但
CCL營收規模較大，所以獲利貢獻金額超過玻纖。

持續漲價對3Q26、4Q26利潤率是很重要因素，但除了漲價之
外 ， 產能利用率拉高 ， 固定成本不變 ， 因此訂單及出貨量增加
對利潤率也有一定貢獻， 產品組合改變也很重要， 整體來說 ，
電子材料全產品項的OPM皆持續提升當中。

電子材料產品主要包括： 
I.玻纖布 
南亞的玻纖布產能排名為全球第三，佔南亞的營收提升至 6-
7%，但獲利貢獻位居前列。年產能約5.9億米(以一般布計)，
自用比重原為40%-50%， 但為優先確保下游高階CCL供料無虞 ，
外銷比例已調降至30%-40%(自用仍以市價計價，不影響實質
利潤)，稼動率自6月起持續滿載。受全球嚴重缺料帶動，7-9
月玻纖布價格強勁上揚，預期4Q26動能不減。玻纖布/紗獲利
率為電子材料中最高，2Q26 OPM已達30%-40%以上。

### Page 6

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  6

日系知名同業大廠為玻纖紗 /布領導廠商，將專注於高階市
場，而Low DK1玻纖布市場供不應求 ， 進而尋找南亞進行合作 。
此日本同業大廠提供玻纖紗，而南亞可提供Low DK1產能，製
成玻纖布後再回送日廠做後段處理。

此外，日本同業大廠也有供應Low DK2玻纖紗給南亞，織成布
後製成自家CCL基板可對外銷售，Low DK2 產品預計2026年內
會量產 ， 屬長期技術互惠合作 。 初期量不大 ， 但3Q26起日東紡
供貨量將明顯增加，足以支撐公司M8以上高階基板需求，對
於公司後續CCL發展，以及高階玻纖數量擴大會有一定幫助。

E-Glass部分 ， 南亞表示各類電子與消費型載板 、PCB層數持續
升級，即使終端消費總量持平，對E-Glass的用料需求依然持
續擴大。E-Glass自2025年底起漲勢確立，3Q26價格仍持續上
漲，公司在9月會議中表示，中國同業於年中報價約2.2美元/
米，4Q26期貨已漲至2.5美元/米 ， 公司預期2027年有機會上看
3美元/米，漲價動能依然非常強勁。南亞E-Glass採大窯爐生
產，與特殊玻纖的坩堝製程不衝突。

同業因原料緊缺或利潤考量放棄中低階訂單，客戶大量轉向
原料掌握度極高的南亞，使南亞在中階與消費型基板 (如M4
等)的稼動率維持滿載 ， 南亞不會放棄E-Glass供應 ， 未來將憑
藉原料自給優勢，承接大量同業外溢轉單，充分享受價量齊
揚帶來的超額利潤。

特殊玻纖紗使用白金坩堝設備，包含坩堝設計、冷卻拉絲等
環節有許多細微變化，會影響產品的介電常數。全球高階豐
田織布機排單已至2028-2029年 ， 南亞因為有超前部署織布機
產能 ， 提早下訂排單 ， 自4Q26起每年均有新機台如期到位加入
生產。透過台灣新港與昆山廠增設坩堝擴產，2026-2028年兩
岸累計將新增達100多個紡位。

高階石英布（Q-Glass）南亞在技術上仍需突破，初期石英紗
會先從外部供應商取得 ， 但因為公司具備生產玻纖紗的經驗 ，
目前還沒放棄，持續積極投入自主研發。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  7

II.銅箔 
南亞的銅箔產能排名全球第二，近期銅箔佔南亞營收已超過
10%。銅箔年產能約為11萬多噸，目前產能利用率滿載，自用
比例約50%。公司計畫在惠州擴建銅箔產線，將增加2.34萬噸
產能 ， 約擴張2成 ， 預計於2028年1月投產 。 台灣彰濱地區也有
銅箔擴產規劃，主要針對PCB應用為主。

南亞的銅箔目前銷量以HVLP1/2為主 ，HVLP3穩定量產 ， 且具備
HVLP 3.5(介於3與4之間的自有規格)及小量HVLP 4生產能力 ，
另外也有一些特定規格之超薄銅箔已進入量產，HVLP 5 則是
持續開發當中，產品線正積極朝向高階發展。

AI伺服器對超低損耗要求嚴格，高階銅箔結晶晶粒需極細，
製程具物理極限使生產速度大幅變慢，投下相同資源，高階
產出噸數遠低於傳統規格，產生嚴重產能排擠效應。

加工費方面，過去多逐季協商、具價格僵固性， 但自7、8月起
開始出現密集且連續調漲 。3Q26大宗銅箔每噸售價約1.7-1.8
萬美元(含銅價約1.4萬)，加工費約3-4千美元/噸。因結構性
生產降速與高階需求不可逆，預期加工費將具備永久性上升
動能，後續極有機會突破4-5千美元/噸。

III.銅箔基板(CCL) 
南亞的銅箔基板 （CCL） 產能排名全球第三 ， 目前CCL兩岸產能
約618萬張/月，台灣 ：中國崑山與惠州約3：7， 目前稼動率滿
載，公司表示1H26 M4 以上基板銷量逾200萬張。台灣樹林與
新港廠區既有設備的小幅改造去瓶頸進度超前，已提前於
4Q26初實現30萬張產能提升 ， 約+5%YoY， 將全數投入M7以上高
階訂單。

CCL佔南亞的營收比重已於 7月達30%左右，M系列佔其中的
2/3(約整體營收20%)，M4佔M系列的營收60%(約CCL營收40%，
約整體營收12%)，其他規格(M6至M8)則是整體營收8%。

### Page 8

請參見末頁重要批露及免責聲明  8

南亞CCL具備M4至M9等級量產能力，其中，M4是出貨量最大產
品，主要供應NVIDIA用於RTX 50 系列顯卡。1H26 M6-M8處於
客戶開發與送樣階段 ， 與多位知名終端客戶與CSP客戶積極洽
談大單，預計4Q26協商將陸續完成，並將迎來明顯放量(合約
會要求客戶提供保障資金)，產品組合升級亦帶動營收、獲利
明顯提升 。M9目前則是已具量產能力並進入客戶認證測試中 ，
數據回饋理想且有極小量試賣，預計大批量產時程將落在  
1H27。

CCL自4Q25起CCL每月維持3-5%漲幅，7、8月部分客戶補漲達
10-15%，4Q26將延續逐月調漲步調 。 南亞不放棄利潤依然可觀
且客戶轉單持續湧入的M4，採 「高中低階全線掌握」 ，藉由產
品組合優化大幅推升CCL獲利率 （2Q26 OPM近20%，3Q、4Q26將
進一步追趕目前玻纖的40% OPM水準）。

儘管目前台光電在M9市佔率最高，然南亞的策略是透過開發
M10等高階產品來實現「彎道超車」，一舉超越競爭對手。南
亞的M10採用自製配方，即K2特殊玻纖布搭配HVP4銅箔，以及
自研的碳氫樹脂，不需仰賴昂貴且進口申請繁瑣的石英布方
案，在未來的成本控制與產能擴張上，將具備極佳的競爭優
勢。M10目前則是還在認證中，實測Dk/Df數據已達M10理想水
準且優於同業 ， 因測試需時1-2千小時 。 整體認證持續推進中 ，
主控權在客戶端 ， 希望1H27取得更具體的驗證成果 ， 若認證順
利通過的話，2H27將逐漸放量。

5. 預估3Q26稅後EPS 4.28元，+942.7%YoY 
受惠於AI發展帶動中高階電子材料供應緊缺、價量齊揚，且
中東戰事帶動化工、聚酯、塑膠加工等產品售價隨原料行情
飆升 ， 南亞2Q26營收合計836.5億元 ，+21.9%QoQ，+27.3%YoY，
營收達成率97%， 符合預期 。 營業利益113.6億元 ，+203.5%QoQ，
+772%YoY， 主要受惠於AI產業擴大投資 ， 相關材料需求持續成
長，使電路板等電子材料產品利潤提高。而權益法投資(台塑
化、南亞科、台朔重工、麥寮汽電、台塑烯烴(美國))共認列
獲利190.9億元(原估145億元)， 優於預期 。2Q26歸屬母公司淨
利267.5億元(原估183.2億元)， 獲利達成率146%， 優於預期 ，
稅後EPS 3.37元(原估2.31元)，+87.7%QoQ，YoY虧轉盈。

展望2H26，雖預期3Q26聚酯部門因紡織用途市況不佳 ， 營收看
季減 ， 化工部門營收看持平 ， 然而 ， 塑膠加工部門營收穩健季
增，且公司看好AI算力規模於未來幾年內仍會大幅成長，邊
緣運算將帶動終端電子材料全系列產品升級，高階材料銷售
比重逐漸提高，電子材料將帶動南亞整體營收將持續向上攀
升，預期9月營運表現將優於8月，3Q26優於2Q26，4Q26優於
3Q26。。研究部預估3Q26營收924.3億元(原估877.9億元)，
+10.5%QoQ ，+44%YoY ， 營 業 利 益144.3 億元， +27.1%QoQ ，
+1,293%YoY ，稅後淨利 339.5 億元 ( 原估 268.4 億元 ) ，
+26.9%QoQ，+943%YoY，稅後EPS 4.28元(原估3.38元)。

6. 預估 2026 、2027 年稅後 EPS 13.82 元、 21.64 元 ， 分 別
+2,325%YoY、+56.6%YoY  
展望未來，研究部認為南亞電子材料事業持續增溫，公司整
體營運將進一步轉佳。預估 2026年營收3,416.8億元(原估
3,287.9億元)，+31.5%YoY，毛利率20.4%，+12.1pctYoY。全
年營業利益478.3億元，+1,192%YoY，本業營運明顯回升，加
上權益法認列轉投資子公司獲利大增 ， 預估2026全年稅後EPS 
13.82元(原估12.02元)，+2,325%YoY。

預估2027年營收4,533億元(原估3,788億元)，+32.7%YoY，毛
利率30.9%，+10.5pctYoY。全年營業利益1,099.5億元(原估
922億元)，+129.8%YoY， 本業營運持續提升 ， 權益法認列轉投
資子公司獲利進一步擴大 ， 預估2027全年稅後淨利1,717億元
(原估1,344億元)，+56.6%YoY，EPS 21.64元(原估16.94元)。

### Page 1

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
Upgrade to Buy: Superior Margin on Tanker Fleet + High Export Ratio
CITI'S TAKE 
We upgrade FPCC from Neutral to Buy reflecting its unique exposure with 
in-house VLCC shipping fleet covering ~60-70% of oil transportation (least 
impacted by recent spot freight rate spike) and highest diesel export ratio 
( ~85% vs. 60% for S-Oil/SKI and 5-10% for TOP/SPRC). We see manageable 
impact from CPC’s pump price freeze (minimal margin squeeze at ~$80/bbl 
Dubai) while FPCC is also a beneficiary of any potential US diesel export 
restriction (Iink). We expect FPCC to offer the highest 26-27E dividend yield 
( ~ 7-8%) among 4 Formosa sisters and a premium valuation seems justified 
vs. Asian peers. We think oil product tightness might take longer to resolve 
given extensive Russia refinery outages. New TP at NT$108 (from NT$40). 
Quantifying impact of domestic price cap — Taiwan’s CPC indicates its retail price 
absorption reached NT$22bn cumulative YTD (Fig.1) vs. NT$17bn in end-Jun and we 
estimate ~NT$7bn in Sep alone (similar to Mar peak). FPCC has 22% Taiwan market 
share and 2025 domestic gasoline & diesel volume was 35k/17k b/d or 12% of CDU 
runs subject to price control (vs. ~18% for S-Oil/SKI, 35% for Thai peers). Assuming 
oil price stays at $100 until end-4Q, we estimate 2H26 domestic forgone EBIT could 
be similar to 1H ( ~NT$5bn) or ~US$2/bbl overall GRM drag. But this was more than 
compensated by SG product crack strength with 2025 gasoline/diesel/jet export 
ratios of 59%/86%/99%, respectively.   
Buffered from current $20+ spot VLCC rate — FPCC is one of the very few refiners 
that owns vessels (through 20% stake in Formosa Plastics Marine that has 6 VLCCs). 
We expect it could enjoy stable freight cost under LT time chartering. We also see 
lower concern on crude sourcing from SoH disruption in 3Q noting Jul/Aug refinery 
runs were 88%/92%. But we see pullback into 4Q (mid-70%) on maintenance. 
Improved olefin competitiveness from 2027 — We expect FPCC could raise cracker 
utilisation next year (33% average since 2Q) after partial conversion to ethane feed 
(up to ~400kt pa usage or ~20% of output) with minimal capex. 
Implications — Our revised TP of NT$108 is based on historical peak 2.3x P/BV (core 
book) at ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil given limited free float and MTM for 
affiliates (its 7.9% stake in Nanya Tech is worth NT$14/share based on market price). 
Our sensitivity analysis suggests a $5 diesel crack rise vs. our mid-$30/bbl base 
case will raise FY27E EPS by ~10%. 
n
Oscar Yee AC
+852-2501-2473
oscar.yee@citi.com
Desmond Law
desmond.law@citi.com

In Taiwan, state-owned Chinese Petroleum Corp (CPC) announces retail price
adjustment on a weekly basis based on government’s fuel pricing formula (linked to
70% Dubai / 30% Brent crude basket adjusted for exchange rate) but with higher
absorption amount as oil goes higher. FPCC tends to follow suit. CPC’s domestic
price absorption was minimal in Jun-Jul’26 when Dubai price falls to ~$80/bbl or

below. However, there was a price freeze last week despite crude basket surged
another $10/bbl WoW to $126/bbl.

### Page 7

Formosa Petrochemical (6505.TW)
25 September 2026 Citi Research
7
Formosa Petrochemical
Company description
Formosa Petrochemical (FPCC) is part of the Formosa Group in Taiwan. It
owns a 540,000 b/d refinery, 2.9m tonnes of ethylene capacity from three
naphtha crackers, and a 2,800MW cogeneration power plant. It was set up
when the Taiwan government approved expansion of the private sector into
the oil refining and upstream chemical business.
Investment strategy

We rate Formosa Petrochemical stock a Buy. Despite a likely gradual GRM
normalisation ahead (dependent on Middle East / Russia supply disruptions),
we expect oil product tightness might take longer to resolve vs. crude oil
market. Its gasoline/diesel export ratio was the highest among Asian peers,
which should more than buffer margin squeeze from domestic pump control.
FPCC was the least impacted by recent spot freight rate spike given in-house
VLCC shipping fleet. While we remain concerned about an L-shaped ethylene

cycle in the next 2-3 years, FPCC could enjoy better cost competitiveness as
~20% of olefin cracker feedstock will shift to ethane from early-2027 with
minimal capex. The LT refining outlook could remain solid due to permanent
closures in the US/Europe. Healthy dividend yield (likely to be the highest
among 4 Formosa sisters in FY26E), stable utility earnings and the stock's
relatively low free float (15%) vs. its peers could provide further support.

Valuation
Our NT$108 target price for Formosa Petrochemical is based on historical
peak-cycle 2.3x FY27E P/BV with adjusted book value excluding valuation
gain of affiliates, ~50% premium to Korean peer S-Oil implied from our
TP given limited free float and MTM for affiliates. We use P/BV as our
valuation methodology due to the asset-heavy nature of the business, and
factor in its scarcity premium vs, regional peers as the only energy play in
Taiwan.

Risks
Key downside risks that could impede the shares from reaching our target
price include: 1) weaker-than-expected oil and/or chemical demand; and 2)
Taiwan government intervention on CPC's oil products pricing mechanism.
Key upside risks are: 1) extended refinery outages in the Middle East / Russia,
2) stronger global oil demand growth; 3) robust chemical re-stocking in
China; and 4) a short-term spike in refining margins on any unscheduled
shutdowns or for weather-related reasons (e.g., cold winter).

Prepared for Kevin Lu

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds
15% against the direction of the call (unless over-ridden by the analyst). The analyst may also remove a Catalyst Watch or STV
call prior to the end of the specified period in a published research note. A Catalyst Watch/STV Upside or Downside call may be

our ratings distribution table for our Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside rating system. However, we reiterate that we do
not consider No View to be a recommendation. For all Catalyst Watch/STV Upside/Downside calls, risk exists that the
catalyst(s) and associated share-price movement will not materialize as expected.

AVC (3017.TW): Liquid cooling rising adoption along with new AI servers 
across GPU and ASIC to drive sequential monthly revenues growth; Buy
25 September 2026 | 7:51PM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect AVC’s monthly revenues to continue 
sequential growth through Dec 2026, leading 3Q26 / 4Q26 revenues to grow at 
+18% / +27% QoQ, driven by (1) new AI server racks ramp up, driving overall liquid 
cooling penetration rate. As we highlighted in June (report link), we expect AI server 
rack (NVL72-equivalent) shipment (incl. Nvidia and AMD) to reach 55k/ 105k/ 163k 
in 2026-28E, increasing by 16% / 20% in 2027 / 28E, reflecting our positive view on 
AI server racks, (2) its major ASIC AI server customer’s new model ramp up, driving its 
air-cooling and liquid-cooling components, along with a rising liquid cooling 
penetration rate, (3) its customer base expansion to a new ASIC AI server, to further 
drive its liquid cooling components in 4Q26, and (4) increasing adoption rate of 
ultra-thin VC in consumer electronics to fit the lightweight design and major 
customer’s foldable phone model. We expect AVC’s net income to grow at a +39% 
CAGR in 2026-28E, riding on the strong AI spending, a growing liquid cooling 
penetration rate, and AVC’s leading market position in total solutions (air cooling, 
liquid cooling with various components / systems). Maintain Buy.

Earnings revisions: We factor in AVC’s Aug revenue and raise our 2026-28E net

Price Target Risks and Methodology - AVC 
We are Buy-rated on AVC. Our 12-month target price for AVC is based on a near-term 
P/E, consistent with our Taiwan Technology coverage. We base our target price of 
NT$4,513 on a target P/E multiple of 30.3x applied to our forward-year EPS (2027E). 
Our target P/E is derived from the correlation between P/E and EPS growth of its peers 
in technology hardware.  
Key downside risks: (1) slower-than-expected adoption of liquid cooling; (2) 
lower-than-expected demand for AI servers; (3) fiercer-than-expected competition; 
and (4) a slower-than-expected recovery in general servers.

Ratings, coverage universe and related definitions
Buy (B), Neutral (N), Sell (S) Analysts recommend stocks as Buys or Sells for inclusion on various regional Investment Lists. Being assigned a Buy or Sell
on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an
Investment List with an active rating (i.e., a  stock that is not Rating Suspended, Not Rated, Early-Stage Biotech, Coverage Suspended or Not Covered), is

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.
Coverage Universe: A list of all stocks in each coverage universe is available by primary analyst, stock and coverage universe at

https://www.gs.com/research/hedge.html.

Asus (2357.TW): AI server racks in expansion along with PC product mix 
upgrade; Aug beat; Neutral
27 September 2026 | 10:33AM HKT
3-month revenues preview: We expect sequential revenue growth in Sep, leading 
3Q26 revenues to grow at 8% QoQ, while we expect sequential revenues decline in 
4Q26 at 7%, considering the PC consumption pull in under the high memory costs 
along with AI servers model transition. We remain positive on the company’s 
expansion toward AI servers and expect the upcoming new models carrying higher 
ASP to further drive its growth in late 2026 / early 2027. We continue to expect 
double digit revenues YoY in 3Q26 / 4Q26E at 39% / 27% YoY driven by PC product 
mix upgrade and AI server racks ramp up. The company retains strong commitments 
on capacity expansion and diversification, with issuance of US$0.5bn of convertible 
bonds and US$1bn of exchangeable bonds, supporting the expansion. Maintain 
Neutral on fair valuation.

Earnings revision: We factor in ASUS’s Aug revenue and raise our 2026-28E net 
incomes by 4% / 7% / 4%, mainly on higher revenues. 2026-28E revenues are raised 
by 4% / 4% / 4%, mainly on robust AI server growth momentum, and PC product mix 
upgrade along with rising selling price under the high memory costs.

### Page 3

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Asus 
We are Neutral-rated on ASUS with a 12-month target price of NT$1,115. Our target 
price is based on 14.8x 2027E P/E, which is derived from brand makers’ average ratio of 
2027E P/E to 2028E NI YoY and OPM. We see EPS growth and OPM as major factors for 
the stock’s share performance.  
Key upside/downside risks include: 1) stronger / weaker-than-expected PC market 
growth; 2) faster / slower-than-expected AI and gaming PC growth; and 3) faster / 
slower-than-expected AI server ramp-up.

## Document 082: 260928_ubs_n2-TAM

Source PDF: all report pdf/260928_ubs_n2-TAM.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-09-28
Pages: 16

### Page 1

ab 28 September 2026
Global Research
UBS Global I/O Semiconductors
Foundry: Much larger N2 TAM and faster A14
expansion; mature foundry upcycle underway
N2 demand inflection larger than expected
Our proprietary bottoms-up N2 foundry TAM analysis points to substantially larger
demand of 320kwpm by 2028E, versus our prior estimate of approximately 230kwpm a
year ago. The increase is driven primarily by stronger server CPU demand and
accelerating AI compute deployments, particularly as a growing number of ASIC

programs ramp into volume production alongside GPUs over the next several years. At
the same time, we  expect Intel to migrate a larger portion of its PC CPU production to
18A as execution improves. As a result, we estimate server CPUs and accelerators will
account for 54% of N2 foundry demand by 2028, up from our previous estimate of
44%, while PCs should contribute 19% and smartphones/tablets 26%. (See Figure 6Q u a n t if y in g  N 2  d e m a n d  o p p o r t u n it ie s  f o r  f o u n d r y  -

Figure 7N 2  f o u n d r y  d e m a n d  b y  a p p lic a t io n  (2 0 2 8 E )). The larger N2 opportunity supports robust revenue growth for TSMC through
2028-29E as the industry's major foundry supplier, even as competition from Samsung
Foundry, Intel, and Terafab increases modestly.
Further acceleration of N3/2 expansion; quicker A14 capacity build-up
To meet the larger N2 demand outlook, we raise our estimate for industry N2 capacity at
end-2028 to 312kwpm, up from 230kwpm previously. We forecast TSMC's N2 capacity

reaching 210kwpm in 2028, versus our prior estimate of 160kwpm. Intel should also
scale capacity more aggressively, exceeding 60kwpm in 2028E for internal CPU
production, compared with our previous forecast of 40kwpm. Meanwhile,
strengthening cloud AI demand in 2027-28E is pushing our industry N3 capacity
forecast to 290-300kwpm by 2028, up from 270kwpm previously, driven entirely by
faster expansion at TSMC. TSMC's A14 node remains on track for mass production in

2028, supported by steady R&D progress and growing customer engagement. Industry
feedback suggests TSMC is pulling forward its expansion schedule, targeting
approximately 60kwpm of A14 capacity in 2028 when the node enters mass
production, based on  UBSe. Reflecting the more aggressive leading-edge expansion, we
raise our TSMC capex forecast to US$90bn in 2027 and US$105bn in 2028, up from US
$63bn this year. See our Q326 earnings preview.
Mature foundry: favorable supply/demand and pricing outlook in 2027-28

We were early in advocating the anti-consensus view of a mature foundry upcycle,
driven by an industry-wide supply and resource reallocation. Utilization and pricing
trends have continued to improve throughout 2026, and we expect more meaningful
earnings upside in 2027-28. Our updated analysis suggests 8-inch foundry utilization
could rise to 95% in 2027, up from 85% in 2026, supported by growing server PMIC
demand. While 12-inch 28/40nm demand may face near-term headwinds from weaker

smartphone and consumer demand, we still expect utilization to improve to 88% in
2027 from 84% in 2026, supported by modest capacity growth. We also believe
competitive behaviour from Chinese foundries should remain rational into 2027, as
major players increasingly prioritize capital allocation toward leading-edge technologies.
Stock recommendations
We reiterate Buy on TSMC and expect further upward revisions to Street revenue, EPS
and capex estimates for 2027-28. Within mature foundry, UMC remains our top pick, as

Valuation Method and Risk Statement
The semiconductor industry is cyclically sensitive to the economy and so upside and downside
risks relate to macro drivers. Other, semiconductor capital equipment specific downside risks
include the rapid loss of market share due to product timing/performance , design or
distribution. The supply chain companies into the handset industry also operate in a highly
cyclical market both from macro concerns and inventory cycles within the supply chain. These

companies also operate in a highly competitive environment with product innovation and
R&D investments critical to success.

KEY DEFINITIONS:Forecast Stock Return (FSR) is defined as expected percentage price appreciation plus gross dividend yield over
the next 12 months. In some cases, this yield may be based on accrued dividends. Market Return Assumption (MRA) is defined as
the one-year local market interest rate plus 5% (a proxy for, and not a forecast of, the equity risk premium). Under Review (UR)
Stocks may be flagged as UR by the analyst, indicating that the stock's price target and/or rating are subject to possible change in the

near term, usually in response to an event that may affect the investment case or valuation. Equity Price Targets have an investment
horizon of 12 months.

such as structure, management, performance record, discount; Neutral: Neutral on factors such as structure, management,
performance record, discount; Sell: Negative on factors such as structure, management, performance record, discount. Core

Investing in structured investments involves significant risks. For a detailed discussion of the risks involved in investing in any particular structured investment, you must read

the relevant offering materials for that investment. Structured investments are unsecured obligations of a particular issuer with returns linked to the performance of an
underlying asset. Depending on the terms of the investment, investors could lose all or a substantial portion of their investment based on the performance of the underlying
asset. Investors could also lose their entire investment if the issuer becomes insolvent. UBS does not guarantee in any way the obligations or the financial condition of any

issuer or the accuracy of any financial information provided by any issuer. Structured investments are not traditional investments and investing in a structured investment is
not equivalent to investing directly in the underlying asset. Structured investments may have limited or no liquidity, and investors should be prepared to hold their investment
to maturity. The return of structured investments may be limited by a maximum gain, participation rate or other feature. Structured investments may include call features and,

if a structured investment is called early, investors would not earn any further return and may not be able to reinvest in similar investments with similar terms. Structured
investments include costs and fees which are generally embedded in the price of the investment. The tax treatment of a structured investment may be complex and may differ
from a direct investment in the underlying asset. UBS and its employees do not provide tax advice. Investors should consult their own tax advisor about their own tax situation

before investing in any securities.

considerations may be lower or higher than portfolios where ESG factors, exclusions, or other sustainability issues are not considered by the portfolio manager, and the

## Document 088: 260929_ms_TDK-Taiyo-Yuden

Source PDF: all report pdf/260929_ms_TDK-Taiyo-Yuden.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-09-29
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

M
Update
Yageo Corp.  | Asia Pacific
TDK-Taiyo Yuden MOU: high-
end wall rises; cycle thesis
intact
The MOU validates AI-driven high-cap MLCC tightness, which
supports pricing and our MLCC up-cycle view.
TDK and Taiyo Yuden (both covered by Shoji Sato) signed an MOU to explore an
alliance in advanced MLCCs/inductors –  including manufacturing/procurement
cooperation, potential METI subsidies under Japan's Economic Security Act, and a
possible future capital tie-up.

Our take: We estimate MLCC demand next year at more than 40bn units of 47uF+,
which crowds out  midrange/low-end MLCC capacity.
1. We believe  de-specing to ≤22uF MLCCs is not a viable workaround.
Substituting high-cap MLCCs with larger counts of lower-capacitance parts
requires broad real estate that simply does not exist on today's densely
packed GPU/accelerator boards.
2. Switching to tantalum-polymer capacitors merely trades one shortage for
another, given equally tight tantalum supply.

3. So the only realistic industry response, in our view, is reallocating midrange/
low-end MLCC capacity toward high-cap production. That would structurally
tightens midrange/low-end supply, underpinning broad-based pricing power.
Things to watch:
• Whether a capital alliance materializes,
• METI subsidy size (this determines how distorted the high-end cost curve
gets),
• Counter-move by Murata (also covered by Shoji Sato) counter-move, and
• Any sign the alliance extends into mid-cap products.

See our latest report on MLCC here: Global Technology: MLCC: Continued Demand
Growth for High-Value-added Products for AI & Data Centers (15 Sep 2026)

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -

### Page 1

ASE Technology Holding

Continued Capex spree underpins strong 
business outlook

Maintain Rating: BUY | PO: 800.00 TWD | Price: 703.00 TWD

YTD equipment buys tracks 82% of its '26 capex guidance 
ASE has filed 152 equipment buys and 101 facility construction announcements through 
late September. The spending on equipment YTD has reached NT$273bn, tracking 82% 
of its '26 capex guidance of US$10.5bn (has revised up from US$8.5bn) not yet 
considering their land and facility purchase. The equipment buy should mainly be for 
2.5D packaging and chip probing, reflecting accelerated capacity expansion amid strong 
demand for its LEAP business from its own project wins and its foundry partner’s 
outsourcing. To secure sufficient facility infrastructure to house additional capacity, ASE 
has announced NT$112bn of spending YTD on facility and clean room construction 
contracts, with these projects expected to progressively complete through 2027-2028.  
Solid sales/margins across the board sponsor heavy capex 
Following ASE’s July announcements to acquire new lands and facilities in Kaohsiung and 
Taoyuan, the company announced another NT$2.15bn facility acquisition in Singapore to 
support the future testing expansion needs in its Singapore subsidiary as its major 
customers request supply base diversification. Despite rising depreciation from heavy 
capex investments and additional funding needs, we forecast ASE’s IC ATM sales to 
+37% CAGR from ‘26-28 while GMs should improve to 33.8%/37% in ‘27/28 (from 
28.3% in 2026) on solid packaging and testing demand for AI chipsets (BofAe LEAP 
sales US$4/10/13bn in 2026/27/28) and fast improving mainstream business outlook.  
Decent earnings support re-rating despite funding needs 
We keep Buy on ASE with NT$800 PO on 20x 2H27-1H28 P/E and maintain EPS for 
'26/27/28E at NT$18/31/45. While additional funding may be required to finance its 
aggressive spending on expansion and R&D projects, we believe the decent customer 
commitment and ROE of 25-30%+ from LEAP business should justify the funding costs.

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
Established in 1984 and headquartered in Taiwan, ASE 
Technology Holding is the no.1 outsourced assembly and 
testing (OSAT) firm globally, in terms of market share by 
revenue. ASE acquired Siliconware Precision (SPIL) in 2018, 
and had acquired USI in 2010, which became its EMS 
business segment providing synergies in system-level 
packaging. 2 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
We rate ASE Buy, factoring in the company's opportunity in 
advanced packaging and testing evidenced in TSMC's 
outsourcing and its own heavy capex investment. The 
strategy shift should allow the company to capture fast 
growing addressable market in high performance 
computing applications and improves its margins. ASE 
could maintain its leading position in the Outsourced 
Semiconductor Assembly and Test (OSAT) universe, owing 
to its well-established footprints and widespread service 
offerings.

### Page 6

Price objective basis & risk 
ASE Technology Holding (XSRIF / ASX) 
We value ASE Technology Holding at NT$800 per share (US$51 for ADR), based on 20x 
2H27-1H28E P/E, above its historical range of 5-26x, in view of its solidified industry 
position in AI era. We use P/E ratio to value ASE as the company has been able to 
maintain healthy profitability through the cycles in the past 20 years due to 
consolidating industry landscape supporting a more benign supply/demand and keeping 
pricing and margins less cyclical.

Downside risks to our PO are 1) share loss and/or ASP erosion owing to Chinese players' 
cannibalization and/or ASE's worsening execution, 2) weakening end demand due to 
uncontrollable matters, such as macro halt and/or geopolitical tensions, 3) regulatory 
issues that could hinder ASE from generating synergies and benefit its competitors.

## Document 107: 261005_ml_TSMC

Source PDF: all report pdf/261005_ml_TSMC.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-10-05

Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co.

TSMC-Musk: customer relationship intact; 
tech/control sharing with Terafab unlikely

Reiterate Rating: BUY | PO: 3,100 TWD | Price: 2,500 TWD

Customer neutrality central to TSMC’s foundry model 
Elon Musk on Saturday October 3rd responded “Just discussions, but something may come 
of it” to an article discussing the potential manufacturing partnership between TSMC 
and Terafab. From chipset design and foundry service perspective, we expect a deeper 
collaboration as Tesla and SpaceX grow at TSMC (2-3% of its sales) directly with their 
auto/robotics and cloud AI ASICs and indirectly through merchant HPC chipset buys (e.g. 
GPUs, networking switches) based on our estimate. However, the likelihood of TSMC 
licensing technology or operating a fab with Terafab holding a meaningful share should 
be low. Specifically, we would note TSMC’s JV fab record is limited including WaferTech, 
SSMC, VIS, JASM, ESMC and AVSM mostly on mature nodes. The company did not co-
own a fab with Apple who contributed ~21% of its revenue from ’15-25 mostly on the 
advanced processes as it keeps full business control and preserves customer neutrality. 
Terafab capex insufficient for 1 terawatt capacity target 
Based on Terafab’s applications under Texas’s JETI program, it outlined ~US$119bn of 
planned investment across 4 phases from ’26-36, with ~60% of it on tool procurement. 
The spending in ’26-28 would be highly skewed toward facility construction before it 
starts to pull in US$6-8bn equipment per annum from ‘29-36. Based on our estimate of 
US$600mn+ for every 1k WPM expansion on the advanced nodes in the US, Terafab 
could only grow its capacity installed base by ~10k WPM per annum at maximum even if 
it can successfully deliver the technology roadmap through its own R&D effort and Intel 
partnership. This would prevent Terafab’s operation from the economies of scale as the 
node migration continues, keeping TSMC as the major foundry partner for Tesla/SpaceX. 
Stock at mid-cycle supported by robust AI demand pipeline 
We keep our Buy rating on TSMC with PO at NT$3,100 on 20x '27 P/E (vs. current 16x).

Company Sector 
Semiconductors

Company Description 
TSMC was founded in 1987 and is listed in Taiwan 
(2330TT) and the US (TSM/NYS).TSMC is the largest and 
global leader in integrated circuit (IC) manufacturing. As a 
build-to-order foundry, it provides a wide range of value-
add activities: IC manufacturing, mask-making, IC design 
services, turnkey solutions, and process development. We 
attribute its success to its proven, winning business model, 
unparalleled scale advantage, optimized execution, and 
technology scope and depth. 5 shares = 1 ADR.

Investment Rationale 
The ongoing semi content growth in mobile, rise of artificial 
intelligence (AI), and proliferation of Internet of Things (IoT) 
should result in sustainable upside in aggregate computing 
power globally. TSMC, as the leading contract manufacturer 
of semiconductor chips, is in a good position to capitalize. 
We are structurally positive on TSMC and expect it to 
sustain over 15% growth with rising FCF from 2026 
onwards.

### Page 4

Price objective basis & risk 
Taiwan Semiconductor Manufacturing Co. (TSMWF / TSM) 
Our PO for TSMC is NT$3,100 per share (US$590 per ADR), based on 20x 2027E P/E. 
TSMC's valuation has traded in the range of 10-26x PE from 2017. 20x is in the mid to 
high end of the historical range, above the average P/E of 18x. We think a PE above the 
average is justified given TSMC's enhanced industry position and mid-40% 5-year CAGR 
in AI should underpin a rerating

Downside risks to our price objective are: (1) greater-than-expected slowdown in global 
smartphone/consumer electronics demand, (2) Intel's potential insourcing strategy and 
ambitions in foundry service, (3) execution risks on advanced nodes, and (4) higher than 
expected tariffs.

We think the capex budget might gradually shift to AI compute
and AI networking in 2028, as memory usage needs to be
optimized. We see positives for TSMC and UMC.
TSMC 3Q26 earnings preview: We expect the company to raise full-year revenue
guidance to the low-to-mid-40% range (vs.  above 40%) Y/Y, implying 4Q26 revenue

will rise 10%-15% Q/Q amid very strong 3nm demand. 3Q26 gross margin should
reach the high end of guidance of 67.5% However, should Intel focus on internal
capacity expansion, we think TSMC would maintain 2nm capacity expansion at the
same pace and its capex at US$54bn for 2026. For 2027, we see TSMC’s capex
reaching US$75bn to US$80bn.
In terms of 2027 wafer price hikes, we see leading edge (7nm and below) up 5%-10%
and mature nodes up 0%-3%; hence, gross margin could approach 70% in 2027. As a

result, we raise  our TSMC price target  by 3% to NT$3,088.
Key topics for upcoming TSMC 3Q26 earnings call on October 15:
1. Would TSMC consider working with TereFab  (Taiwan News, Oct 3 2026) and
building new fab sites in Texas (Bloomberg, Oct 1 2026)? Our view is that expansion
in Texas could be necessary, considering customer demand and limited space at the
Arizona fab in five years.
2. If Intel were to increase capex, would that reduce outsourcing at TSMC? Our

recent industry checks suggest some trimming of Intel’s 3nm and 2nm; how might
TSMC reallocate its resources?
3. How would TSMC address large reticle size chip demand? Our checks suggest
MediaTek handled TPU v10 IceFish reticle size initially at 14x; could TSMC’s CoWoS
or CoPoS be able to support such packaging?
We raise  our UMC price target by 36% to NT$188 on AI networking (SiPh)
business upside: Some investors remain on the sidelines amid ongoing smartphone

and PC demand weakness. We have seen some order cuts in PC semis recently, as
some consumer analog IC customers (e.g., Silergy) have not been able to pass
through UMC’s wafer price hike. Therefore, some investors do not expect any UMC’s
price hike in 2027. However, two growth drivers could help UMC raise wafer price
for 8-inch in 2027: 1) Samsung DDI outsourcing, as we noted in our earlier  Foundry
Floorplan, and 2) growing SiPh PIC demand, in particular after UMC introduced its

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

TSMC 2nm and 3nm capacity growth remains robust; 5nm
set to decline
Consistent with our demand forecast, TSMC indicated very strong capacity requirements
for both 2nm and 3nm starting in 2026, implying a 70% capacity CAGR for N2 from 2026

to 2028. TSMC also suggested that 5nm capacity will begin to decline from 2027, in line
with our forecast that 5nm demand will drop as Blackwell transitions to Rubin on 3nm.
Separately, advanced packaging capacity expansion should remain in focus for TSMC in
2027. This includes, but is not limited to, CoWoS and SoIC, for which we now expect
capacity to reach 200kwpm and 40kwpm, respectively, by 2027.

How might TSMC address Intel's competition in both wafer
foundry and advanced packaging?
Intel EMIB-T remains competitive in 2029; how TSMC will
address?
With demand in AI continuing to boom, we expect TSMC and its OSAT partners to
expedite capacity expansion plans for CoWoS. We see TSMC's CoWoS capacity building
further to 260kwpm in 2028, from 130/200kwpm in 2026/2027, with 2028's expansion
mainly focused in Arizona's AP9/10 and potentially in AP7. TSMC is now conducting trial

production for CoPoS in Taoyuan. If the progress goes well, we would expect TSMC to
start building its CoPoS capacity, with location also likely to be in AP9/10 or AP7P3.
We expect the non-TSMC camp to expand CoWoS capacity to 110kwpm by 2028 year-end,
from 80kwpm by end-2027. For ASE/SPIL, we expect FoCoS+CoWoS capacity to rise from
30kwpm at 2027-end to 50-60kwpm at 2028-end, with expansion focused on CoWoS-L
and CoWoS-R. For Amkor, we expect CoWoS capacity to increase from 30kwpm at 2027-

end to 55-60kwpm at 2028-end, also focused on CoWoS-L and CoWoS-R.

What about  Intel EMIB capacity build?
For Intel, we see current average EMIB-M capacity of 110kwpm falling slightly to 95kwpm
in 2027 as it converts some toward EMIB-T. We see the major customers of EMIB-M being
Trainium3, upcoming Trainium 4 and its in-house server CPU. Average EMIB-T capacity is

around 5kwpm in 2026, which we expect to further scale to 15-20kwpm in 2027 and 40-
45kwpm in 2028. All of the capacity mentioned above is converted for  12-inch wafer
equivalent, which we believe would be much more easier to communicate with compared
to TSMC CoWoS capacity.
Although some small projects are ongoing, we still see MediaTek/Google Humufish
consuming most of the capacity during 2027-28. We maintain our view that Intel EMIB-T

Indeed, we have seen some TSMC customers surveying (testing vehicles) Intel EMIB-T due
to the design for larger chip size, lower cost, and intention to seek a second source
because TSMC's CoWoS capacity is still relatively tight. However, we believe TSMC could
still try to expand its packaging size by leveraging different technologies to meet
customers' needs. We think it remains concerned about whether it is going to open its
front-end computing die production/know-how to its competitor, Intel, for back-end

packaging.
Our prediction of the trend after 2028 –  TSMC might:
1. Pull in CoPoS (panel-level process) with both 300mm and 500mm square panel
size
2. Introduce 3.5D packaging with SoIC for vertical chip stacking (Meta MTIA is
surveying)
3. Adopt glass core substrate to solve warpage for >9.7x reticle-size chips

Pros and cons of TSMC's CoWoS, Intel's EMIB, TSMC's
CoPoS, and SoIC
TSMC’s CoWoS, Intel’s EMIB, TSMC’s CoPoS, and TSMC’s SoIC represent different points
along the advanced packaging spectrum, each optimizing for a different balance of
bandwidth, cost, scalability and integration complexity. We think future design of the
chips is likely to combine these approaches—for example, using CoWoS for lateral

integration and SoIC for vertical stacking—to achieve optimal system-level performance.
Exhibit 12: Comparison between TSMC's CoWoS, CoPoS, SoIC, and Intel's EMIB-T

In summary,

### Page 9

M
Idea
Morgan Stanley Research 9
• CoWoS excels in high-bandwidth 2.5D integration but is costly and capacity-limited
• EMIB offers a more cost-effective and modular alternative with some performance
trade-offs
• CoPoS targets large-scale, cost-efficient integration but is still maturing
• SoIC delivers the highest performance through 3D stacking at the expense of
complexity and thermal constraints.

### Page 10

M
Idea
10
UMC is well positioned for Silicon Photonic IC (PIC) foundry
In our previous Foundry Floorplan, we presented a constructive view on UMC given its AI-
related revenue. Management indicated AI revenue of US$300mn this year (3-5% of
revenue), potentially more than tripling to US$1bn in three years (2029).
UMC has a  capacity expansion plan to capture AI demand, including a clean room for
advanced packaging for AI chips, such as wafer-on-wafer and memory-on-memory stacking.

Some investors remain on the sidelines amid ongoing smartphone and PC demand
weakness, and we have seen some order cuts of PC semis recently. Some think a UMC
price hike in 2027 wouldn’t go through, but we see two growth drivers helping it
successfully  hike wafer price for 8-inch in 2027: 1) Samsung DDI outsourcing, and 2)
growing SiPh PIC demand, in particular after UMC introduced its 400Gb per lane solution
(PIC + TFLN) solution.
How UMC is differentiated in PIC foundry

According to UMC, InP and TFLN are the two most promising solutions, as they are much
more mature than the other contenders. InP is facing severe shortage, adding to TFLN’s
momentum.
UMC Group’s TFLN program started in 2021 with 6” in mass production and 8” in
sampling. TFLN's   strengths include low loss, high bandwidth (> 110 GHz), good thermal
stability, and strong linearity.
Integrating TFLN with PIC is a challenge as well as an opportunity. Advanced packaging

schemes and micro transfer printing are both seen as promising candidates. UMC is
developing the integration technology, leveraging its expertise in PIC, TFLN, and advanced
packaging.
Adoption of higher-performance modulators (TFLN/InP) and improvement in photo diodes
are key factors affecting deployment of plain-vanilla (non-WDM, IM/DD) DCIs with 400
Gbps/channel performance and beyond, which is very close to TSMC's mainstream PIC
technology Micro Ring.

Exhibit 15: UMC differentiates in TFLN process offering for PIC
Source: UMC

About Foundry Floorplan
In 2011,  we first published this product (Asia/Pacific Foundry: The Foundry Floorplan) to
provide our analysis of foundry industry dynamics. We have resumed this practice as
global foundry competition remains active. Foundry supply is also a critical resource for
future AI chips, whether in the global market or China's local market.
Below are prior reports from this series:
• The Foundry Floorplan: All boats riding on the same tide – mature node up-cycle

begins (19 May 2026)
• The Foundry Floorplan: TSMC preview update; CoWoS vs. EMIB competition (14
Apr 2026)
• The Foundry Floorplan: Raise TSMC's 2027e and 2028e Capex (9 Mar 2026)
• The Foundry Floorplan: Update on TSMC's 2027 capex; interposer outsourcing to
Vanguard? (13 Jan 2026)
• Asia/Pacific Foundry: The Foundry Floorplan, April 2011

TSMC: Estimate and price target revisions
Our earnings forecasts increase 4% for 2026,  5% for  2027 and 3% for 2028: We raise
our 2026 revenue forecast and modestly increase our margin assumptions to reflect
stronger-than-expected 3Q26 performance, driven by robust AI demand, including
sustained Blackwell shipments. We raise our 2027–28 revenue assumptions by 3%–5%,
reflecting the increase in strong leading-edge demand as well as the wafer price hike.

Our PT increases to NT$3,088 from   NT$2,988, driven primarily by our estimate

revisions. Our PT implies a 20x P/E multiple on our new 2027 EPS forecast. TSMC
currently trades at 17x that estimate, close to its average NTM P/E of 16.5x since 2018. We
find the stock attractive at current levels.
We continue to derive our 12-month price target (base-case scenario) using a residual
income valuation framework. Our key assumptions are unchanged, including a cost of
equity of 9.2% (beta of 1.2, equity risk premium of 6.0%, and risk-free rate of 2.0%), an

intermediate growth rate of 10.5%, and a terminal growth rate of 4%.
Our bull and bear case scenario values increase to NT$3,720 (from NT$3,600) and NT
$1,700 (from NT$1,645), respectively.

NT$3,720.00
24x 2027e EPS
TSMC dominates foundry services: 1)
Breakthrough in EUV tech and materials
accelerates node migration to 3nm and
beyond. 2) Intel outsources its server CPU
production to TSMC sooner than expected.
3) Intel or Samsung exits leading-edge
foundry business. 4) New tech megatrends
such as 6G or AI drive global semi revenue

growth and increase demand for leading
edge nodes. 5) Cost per transistor decreases
further along with node migration.
NT$3,088.00
20x 2027e EPS
Leading position in leading-edge logic
foundry services with some gross margin
expansion: 1) Global semi revenue CAGR is
sustained in the high single digits in 2025-
28. 2) TSMC continues to lead in market
share in 16nm, 7nm, 5nm, 3nm, and 2nm. 3)
It continues to observe robust AI-related
demand. 4) Cost per transistor does not

decrease meaningfully in 3nm and 2nm,
while capex per k is more demanding than
for 7nm and 5nm.
NT$1,700.00
11x 2027e EPS
TSMC's dominance in foundry business
erodes: 1) Global ex-memory semi revenue
growth is weaker than expected in 2025-28.
2) Declining trailing-edge market share
causes revenue shortfall. 3) Intel's
contribution is weaker than expected. Intel
and/or Samsung successfully develop
leading-edge process technology. 4) Intel's
foundry business execution proves

successful and weighs on TSMC with order
competition. 5) Demand for 2nm is less than
expected given high transistor cost for
foundry customers.

UMC: Estimate and price target revisions
We raise our 2026, 2027, and 2028 earnings estimates by 56%, 25%, and 31%,
respectively: Our 2026e change is due mostly to strong 2Q26 non-op gains. We increase
2027/28e revenue and EPS due to new CPO related business contribution from 12-inch
SiPh PIC  as well as 8-inch TFLN. Our 2028e increase also includes DDI outsource from
Samsung foundry.

▪ We are OW as we see structural changes
in the mature node industry's
supply/demand dynamics.
▪ Its foundry quality is comparable to
TSMC's in mature nodes. Along with

foundry peers.

should be one of UMC's long-term

Stabilized semi demand; better-than-

demand: 1) Global semi sales grow >15% Y/Y

in 2026, and ex-TSMC foundry industry

NT$188.00
5.2x 2027e BVPS
Healthy semi growth; improving demand for
8-inch and trailing-edge 12-inch: 1) Global
semi sales grow ~15% in 2026 and ex-TSMC

foundry industry grows 3-5% Y/Y; 2) 8-inch
utilization rate is above 75% with steady 12-
inch demand amid semi inventory digestion;
3) UMC hikes wafer prices slightly in
response to strong AI-related demand.
NT$95.00
2.6x 2027e BVPS
Signiﬁcant semi industry decline; 8-inch and
trailing-edge 12-inch face weakening
demand: 1) Global semi sales grow only <5%
in 2026; 2) display semi and power semi
demand deteriorates further; 3) signiﬁcant
wafer pricing erosion given low fab

utilization and mature node peers'
competition; 4) depreciation ramp-up of
more than 30% Y/Y in 2026 without
sufﬁcient wafer order inﬂow to sustain
UMC's gross margin.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

hold, and sell but represent recommended relative weightings (see definitions below). To satisfy regulatory requirements, we correspond Overweight, our most positive stock rating, with a
buy recommendation; we correspond Equal-weight and Not-Rated to hold and Underweight to sell recommendations, respectively.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views

below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

營收走勢圖

觀光事業  
八方雲集(2753.TT) 
台灣穩健成長，美國快餐新模式開啟新引擎

研究部預估八方雲集 2026 年 EPS 14.07 元(+12.2%YOY)，2027 年
15.05 元(+9.5%YOY)，對八方營運仍持正面看法，主要是考量：(1)下半
年肉品價格有所回落 ，成本壓力減輕， 加上公司嚴格控管美國地區費用，
下半年營業利益將能改善 ；(2)美國市場仍是公司中長期業績成長的主要
動能。儘管德州門市延後至今年 9 月才開幕，但快餐新模式初期單店營
收與營運效率均優於預期，有望改善美國事業營運業績。研究部給予 15
倍本益比，目標價 225 元，維持買進評等。

1. 雙品牌展店穩步推進，台灣營運維持成長 
八方雲集與梁社漢持續在台灣展店，截至2026 年 8 月底門市分別達
1,058 家及 260 家 ， 較去年底淨增25 家及 12 家 。 既有門市表現亦穩
健，1～8 月八方與梁社漢同店銷售分別成長約 5%及 6%。 未來公司將
持續開發科技園區、辦公大樓及封閉型商圈，並擴大 To Go 小型店
型，以提高市場滲透率。研究部預期台灣區 2026 年、2027 年營收將
分別成長 8.3%YoY 與 5.0%YoY。

2. 美國市場展店增加，營運有望改善 
美國事業目前仍處於快速擴張期 ，2025 年底總店數達 13 家，2026 年
上半年在加州新增 3 家門店，9 月底達拉斯開幕 ，之後德州還有 2 家
門店預計在年底前開出，預期至 2027 年將至少新增 5 家，屆時明年
底總家數有望達到 25 家。研究部預估美國區今明兩年營收都將有約
40%的增速，令2027 年美國事業虧損幅度收斂，並有望於 2028 年實
現年度損平。

3. 快餐新模式初步驗證成功，具跨區複製潛力 
達拉斯快餐新模式不僅提高出餐速度 ， 也可簡化人力與教育訓練 ， 初
期營運表現優於公司原先預期 。 公司計畫將此模式複製至美國新店 ，
甚至改造部分效率較低的加州門市，有望成為未來美國展店與改善
業績的重要模式。

一、營運概況：

八方雲集仍以台灣市場為公司主要營運重心 ， 美國市場則是逐步提高營收
占比。上半年品牌營收比重分別為：八方雲集 74.7%、梁社漢 24.8%、芳
珍 0.4%、丹堤 0.1%。

台灣市場：雙品牌展店提速，數位點餐創造更多客流 
台灣市場為八方雲集最重要的營運與獲利支柱 。受惠於雙品牌規模經濟發
酵 與 中 央 工 廠 效 益 提 升， 台 灣 地 區 1H26 營收 上 升 至 35.60 億元
(+7.1%YoY)，獲利5.98 億元(+22.3%YoY)，獲利增速顯著優於營收。這波
成長動能延續到下半年。截至 2026 年 8 月底，主品牌「八方雲集」台灣
門市達 1,058 家，較去年底淨增 25 家； 「梁社漢排骨」達260 家，較去年
底淨增 12 家，其中加盟店占比已提升至 84%。

台灣市場仍是 八方雲集 主要營運
重心，上半年台灣區營收比重達
77.5%

八方雲集在台灣市場的餐飲品牌
以加盟店較多。海外市場則是以
直營店為主。

此外，數位化轉型效益逐步顯現，線上點餐系統於加速導入後，有效縮短
尖峰時間的候餐期 ， 並提升門市整體服務量能。在今年尚未調漲售價的前
提下 ，1～8 月八方雲集與梁社漢的同店銷售增長率分別達 5%與 6%， 顯示
成長動能主要來自來客數與客單價的雙重提升，整體營運維持穩健。

美國市場：業務進軍德州，營收增速保持強勁 
美國市場自 2022 年 3 月以南加州為起點，設立加州中央工廠，並以直營
方式陸續展店， 已成為八方雲集目前最重要的海外擴張重心 ， 隨著新店持
續開出，營運規模正快速放大。在總門店家數由去年底的 13 家擴增至 16
家的帶動下，美國地區 1H26 營收則提升至 4.38 億元(+51.7%YoY)，展現
強勁的成長動能。

位於德州的達拉斯首店於 9 月正式開幕，將全美營運門市推升至 17 家。
目前德州另有 2 家門市正在裝修，預計年底前開出，2026 年底總店數至
少達到 19 家，若其餘送審門市進度順利，更有機會衝刺 20 家大關。

截至 2026 年 8 月底，主品牌「八
方雲集」台灣門市達 1,058 家，

### Page 4

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 4

受固定成本與前期投入影響，1H26 美國營運尚處小額虧損階段 
美國子公司 1H26 虧損 5,700 萬元，主要是因為營收規模尚不足以完全吸
收固定成本，相關影響包括：(1)加州有額外成本支出。管理層表示，去
年北加州門市開出後，加州區域營運已接近損平，但 1H26 有 3 家新店延
後設計及送審，造成近 1 千萬元的前期支出；(2)為取得跨州供應資格，
加州中央工廠導入 USDA 規範，初期生產排程受限，且清潔流程、人力配
置要求提高，在生產效率下降時，成本亦同步增加。管理層表示，USDA 規
範導入初期的磨合階段已大致完成，目前生產效率正逐步改善 。(3)在達
拉斯首店於 9 月開幕前，持續投入的前期人力、租金等籌備支出。

德州廠產能到位，聚焦 USDA 認證與跨州物流戰略 
德州中央工廠已完工並正式投入生產，目前產能約可支援 30 至 50 家門
市 ， 因此短期內美國沒有重大資本支出的規劃。之後德州中央工廠亦將申
請 USDA 認證資格，屆時該廠食材將能採跨州冷凍配送供應至其他門店。
沿德州至加州物流路線積極洽談新店點(如在亞利桑那、 內華達等)，進一
步加快展店速度並產生規模經濟。

圖五、八方雲集美國區季度營收變化

資料來源：法說資料

香港市場：景氣偏弱策略審慎，精簡門市汰弱留強 
香港市場受當地消費景氣偏弱影響 ， 近兩年營運策略轉向審慎 ， 展店重心
轉為汰弱留強， 使香港地區1H26 營收下滑至 5.98 億元(-11.1%YoY)，1H26
獲利亦下滑至 1,570 萬元(-16.0%YoY)。雖然仍維持獲利，但受到門市營
收下滑及高租金壓力影響 ，利潤空間受到擠壓 。此次調整主要是關閉租金
成本過高的門市，故截至 2026 年 8 月底，集團在香港各品牌門市合計下
滑至 71 家，較 2025 年底淨減少 7 家。

二、業績回顧：

1H26 業績依舊有偏強增速，惟 2Q26 毛利率因成本壓力而下降 
八方雲集 2026 年上半年合併營收達 45.96 億元，成長 7.3%YoY；1H26 營
業利益達 5.56 億元，成長 15.4%YoY；1H26 稅後淨利 4.67 億元，成長
20.5%YoY；1H26EPS 7.01 元 。獲利增速高於營收表現，主要是受惠台灣業
務的規模經濟顯現 、 自動化中央工廠效益提升，令1H26 毛利率升至36.0%，
年增 0.4 個百分點；費用率則降至 23.9%，年減 0.5 個百分點。

單季度來看 ， 八方雲集2Q26 合併營收23.33 億元(+3.1%QoQ，+10.7% YoY)；
營業利益 2.72 億元(-4.0%QoQ，+13.0%YoY)，稅後淨利 2.30 億元(-
2.9%QoQ，+19.4%YoY)，2Q26EPS 3.45 元，略低於 1Q26EPS 3.56 元。

香港地區 1H26 營收下滑至 5.98
億元(-11.1%YoY)，1H26 獲利亦下
滑至 1,570 萬元(-16.0%YoY)。

獲利較前季下滑，主要是因為：(1)公司未調漲台灣區售價，但肉品價格
上漲，包材及運輸成本亦上升，壓縮利潤空間；(2)加州中央工廠因導入
USDA 規範而使生產效率下降。工廠需增加清潔程序 、 生產人力 ， 且生產排
程受到更多限制，亦增加部分成本，進而使2Q26 毛利率下降至 35.6%(季
減 0.8 個百分點，年減 0.4 個百分點)。

第 2 季推銷費用金額雖有增加，但因台灣區營收成長較快，2Q26 推銷費
用率下降至 17.9%(季減 0.1 個百分點，年減0.9 個百分點)。第2 季管理
費用較去年同期增加約 2 千萬元 ，主要來自德州前期籌備 、 人員進駐等支
出。管理層表示下半年仍會嚴控費用 ，預期費用不會較上半年出現明顯跳
升。

圖八、八方雲集 季度合併營收與毛利率表現

資料來源：法說資料

三、營運展望：

台灣市場：聚焦特定商圈展店，擴大 To Go 新店型深化滲透 
管理層認為台灣市場仍具進一步的展店與滲透空間 ， 未來將持續推進擴張
步伐。在選址策略上，公司將精準鎖定科技園區、辦公大樓、學區及封閉
型商圈進行開發。此外，公司將大力擴大布局「To Go 」小型外帶店。此
店型藉由縮小坪數 ， 能有效降低裝潢與人力成本 ， 不僅能大幅提升加盟主
的加盟意願 ， 更有利於切入過去因高昂租金而難以進駐的精華商圈 。 在門
市穩健增加的動能帶動下 ，研究部預期八方雲集台灣區今明兩年營收將分
別溫和增長 8.3%YoY 與 5.0%YoY。

2Q26 毛利率下降至 35.6%，主要
是因為國內肉品價格 上漲，加上
加州中央工廠額外成本支出。

### Page 7

請參見末頁重要披露及免責聲明 7

美國市場：達拉斯快餐模式初步驗證成效，2027 年展店能見度高 
美國市場展店能見度已成功延伸至 2027 年。目前德州與亞利桑那州鳳凰
城已分別完成 3 家及 2 家 、共計5 家新店的簽約 。 考量美國新店從簽約到
開幕約需 7～8 個月 ， 此5 家門市預計將於 2027 年陸續加入營運 ； 同時 ，
公司也持續在加州北部與聖地牙哥等區域尋找新據點 。 在展店效益持續發
酵下，研究部預估今明兩年美國區營收將維持在 40%左右的強勁增速。

在營運效益上，八方雲集於達拉斯首店首度導入「快餐模式」 ，以批次製
作搭配流水線取餐，每小時可服務高達 100～120 位顧客。試營運期間僅
午餐 3 小時營收便達約 5,000 美元。管理層預估全天營業後 ， 日均營收可
望衝上 1.0 萬至 1.5 萬美元 ， 初期表現顯著優於加州門市的原先假設 。 若
此新模式持續驗證成功，公司計畫進一步導入加州部分門市 。

由於德州中央工廠已於 9 月正式投產 ， 短期美國事業因新店籌備 、USDA 規
範 、 物流及德州中央工廠固定成本先行投入等因素影響， 預期仍會呈現小
額虧損。然而，隨 2027 年展店家數增加、區域門市密度及中央工廠稼動
率提升 ， 固定成本可望逐步攤薄 ，尤其新快餐模式有望降低德州工廠所需
的損益兩平店數，預期2027 年美國事業虧損幅度將能收斂，並有望2028
年實現年度損平。

香港市場：大規模閉店告終，但策略仍謹慎，展望平淡 
香港市場上半年的結構性調整主要為關閉租金成本過高的門市 。 目前這一
波大規模的閉店調整已大致告一段落 ，未來集團不會再積極縮減店數 ， 但
仍會持續檢視單店效益 ，追求單店獲利能力的改善。考量香港市場未來策
略仍偏謹慎保守，故研究部預期香港區營收今年營收衰退 10.0%YoY，明
年衰退 3.0%YoY。

四、獲利預估：

下半年營收逐季升，且成本壓力趨緩，預期本業獲利將改善 
展望 2026 年，台灣「八方雲集」與「梁社漢排骨」展店動能優於預期，
預期台灣區營收仍維持穩健成長。美國方面，9 月達拉斯首店開幕後，還
會再新增 2~3 家門市，將能帶動合併營收逐季提升。雖 2Q26 毛利率受到
肉品、包材成本上漲影響，但隨下半年原物料價格趨穩，加上公司嚴控費
用，盈利能力有望逐步改善。研究部預估 2026 年合併營收 95.20 億元
（+8.3%YoY ） ，毛利率35.9% 、費用率 23.6% ，營業利益 11.73 億元
（+8.8%YoY） ，稅後淨利9.61 億元（+12.2%YoY），EPS 14.07 元。

美國進入加速展店期，新模式效果逐步顯現 
展望 2027 年，台灣市場聚焦八方與梁社漢兩大主力品牌，將繼續拓點，
滲透更多商圈 ； 美國市場得益於門市數持續增加 ， 以及快餐模式可望成為
後續美國展店的新標準，擴大營收規模。研究部預估 2027 年合併營收
102.33 億元 （+7.5%YoY） ，毛利率回升至36.0%，費用率降至23.1%，營業
利益 13.23 億元（+12.8%YoY） ，稅後淨利10.52 億元（+9.5%YoY），EPS 
15.05 元。

五、投資建議：

考量八方雲集台灣本業下半年業績將能改善 ，4Q26 營收與獲利還會創高 ，
加上美國市場營收成長且中期有望實現獲利，研究部給予 15 倍本益比。
目標價 225 元，維持買進評等。

評等  定義  
強力買進     預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬超過 25%以上  
買進  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬 10% 以上  
中立  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 10～-10%  
降低持股  預估未來 6 個月內的絕對報酬介於 (-10%)以下  
未評等  沒有足夠的基本資料判斷該公司評等

提 供 專 屬 之 投 資 建 議。 文 中 所 載 資 訊 或 任 何 意 見，不 構 成 任 何 買 賣 有 價 證 券 或 其
他 投 資 標 的 之 要 約、 宣 傳 或 引 誘 等 事 項。 對 於 本 投 資 報 告 所 討 論 或 建 議 之 任 何 證

周建霖

光環(3234) 
買進, TP 240 (潛在報酬 32.23%)  
買進 
Price(10/02):181.5 
Target Price：240

在完成客戶 5,000 小時可靠度測試認證後，光環代工業務自 26H2
起重新貢獻營運， 隨稼動率提升及產品組合優化， 本業獲利已顯著
改善並順利轉虧為盈 。 為配合客戶後續產能規劃， 公司亦將斥資約
11 億元進行產線擴充，預期至2027 年底產能規模將達 2026 年底
的 3 倍。此外，針對具 Scale-up 應用潛力的 VCSEL 技術，光環
亦規劃推出 1060nm VCSEL 方案供客戶驗證，有望切入新一代
AI 光互連市場，進一步強化公司中長期競爭優勢。綜合以上，研
究部預估光環 2026 年 EPS 0.57 元，2027 年 EPS 6.77 元，目前
PE 為 26.5x，參考光通上游同業 PE 評價為 20-60x，考量光環本
業獲利將開始由虧轉盈且顯著成長，且也開始佈局 Scale-up 雷射
方案，因此給予 35xPER，目標價 240 元，建議買進。 
⚫ 公司基本資料 ⚫ 公司基本資料 
光環(3234)成立於 1997 年，主要產品為光通訊主動元件，包含雷
射晶粒切割、次模組封裝，2025 年晶粒及零組件佔 63%、光電轉
換件 28%、其他 9%。應用領域則為頻寬網路 10%、4G/5G 基地
台互聯 31%、雲端數據中心 22%、感測應用 26%、其他 11%，銷
售地區為台灣 60%、 中國8%、 歐洲及美洲17%、 亞洲及其他15%。

殖利率(%) - - ⚫ 營運概況 
取得客戶測試認證，代工業務重回貢獻 
光環 26Q2 營收 2.0 億元，Q+7.5%，Y+28.1%，毛利率 19.82%，
稅後淨利-1744 萬元，Q 持續虧損，Y 持續虧損，EPS -0.16 元。
在重新取得客戶 5000 小時的測試認證下，代工收入逐步重回貢獻
且產品組合優化下，使整體毛利率回升至雙位數以上，並在費用控
管得宜讓本業獲利虧損顯著收斂。

代工事業加速成長，本業由虧轉盈 
光環 8 月營收 1.1 億元，M+13.1%，Y+110.1%，7 月營收 9604
萬元，M+25.2%，Y+96.9%，7-8 月合計 2.0 億元，Y+103.7%。
代工事業逐月增加片數 ， 研究部認為在稼動率回升及產品組合優化
下，光環獲利將持續獲得顯著改善，其中7 月自結稅後淨利約 900
萬，EPS 0.08 元，反映出本業隨營收的放大而已轉虧為盈。綜合
以 上 ， 研 究 部 預 估 光 環26Q3 營收 3.2 億元， Q+63.9% ，
Y+113.2%，毛利率 33.77%，稅後淨利 4462 萬元，Q 虧轉盈，Y
虧轉盈，EPS 0.35 元。

產能積極布局，同時開發 1060nm VCSEL 自有產品 
光環今年以來已完成 16.5 億元現金增資(稀釋 EPS 約 11.8%)，並
公告將斥資約 11 億元進行產能擴充及設備擴置。研究部推估，此
次擴產主要係配合客戶 2027～2028 年產能規劃 ， 因而提前建置相
對應代工設備，預期至 2027 年底整體產能規模有望達 2026 年底
的 3 倍，且公司亦已著手規劃 2028 年後續擴產計畫。綜上所述，
預期光環代工業務將自 26H2 起加速成長，隨產品組合改善，本業
可望轉為穩定獲利，加以產能利用率提升帶動營運槓桿逐步顯現，
預期整體獲利增幅將優於營收成長。此外，光環深耕VCSEL 市場
多 年 ， 而 今 年 以 來 多 家 國 際 光 通 訊 及 雷 射 大 廠 亦 陸 續 提 出 以

VCSEL 作為 Scale-up 光互連光源的方案 。 相較SiPh 方案 ，VCSEL
具備高頻寬密度、低功耗、低延遲潛力及高可靠度等優勢，過往傳
輸 距 離 較 短 的 限 制 ， 在 以 機 櫃 內 及 機 櫃 間 短 距 離 互 連 為 主 的
Scale-up 應用中影響亦相對有限。研究部認為，若未來 Scale-up
架構朝 Wide-and-Slow 方向發展，VCSEL 將成為極具競爭力的技
術選項。光環憑藉既有技術與量產經驗，目前也開始佈局1060nm
的 VCSEL 方案，預計於年底推出樣品給客戶測試，未來有望成為
強化公司長期競爭優勢的自有產品。綜合以上，研究部預估光環
2026 年營收 11.0 億元 ，Y+60.7%， 毛利率28.29%， 稅後淨利7233
萬元 ，Y 虧轉盈 ，EPS 0.57 元；2027 年營收 28.8 億元 ，Y+162.0%，
毛利率 40.89%，稅後淨利 8.6 億元，Y+1083.9%，EPS 6.77 元。

圖二：Scale-up 互連技術比較

資料來源：Lumentum、第一金投顧彙整

結論 
在完成客戶 5,000 小時可靠度測試認證後，光環代工業務自 26H2
起重新貢獻營運，隨稼動率提升及產品組合優化，本業獲利已顯著
改善並順利轉虧為盈。為配合客戶後續產能規劃，公司亦將斥資約
11 億元進行產線擴充， 預期至2027 年底產能規模將達 2026 年底
的 3 倍。此外，針對具 Scale-up 應用潛力的 VCSEL 技術，光環
亦規劃推出 1060nm VCSEL 方案供客戶驗證 ， 有望切入新一代AI
光互連市場，進一步強化公司中長期競爭優勢。綜合以上，研究部

鈊象 (3293 TT)  Buy

2027 年因 Social SS 帶動，營收展望正向，給予 Buy 建議  
投資建議 ： 2027 年 TaDa Gaming 在美國發展 Social SS 已明顯
看到成效，因基期和市占率仍偏低，故 Social SS 會是未來 1~2
年營收的主要成長動能，考量 2027 年展望正向且評價偏低，給
予 Buy 的投資建議。  
美國的 Social SS 因基期和市占率仍偏低，為未來 1~2 年的主要
成長動能： 展望 2H26~2027 年，區域營收比重方面， 01~08/2026
為東南亞 54%，台灣 16%，中國 5%，美國 14%，其他 11%，若
與 01~02/2026 和 2025 年相比，台灣和中國比重下滑，美國和
其他比重提升，東南亞則持平。以實際營收來看， 01~08/2026 東
南亞仍有 YOY +18% 的成長，美國和其他地區則是 YOY +50%
以上的成長，台灣和中國則為 YOY 衰退。授權遊戲的部分，美
國和其他地區營收大幅成長，主要是 TaDa Gaming 在拓展美國
和中南美市場已明顯看到成效，歐洲和非洲則因為競爭激烈，
仍在努力推廣中。其中美國市場過去是以三大州為主，為過去
常講的 SS 或是美國授權，在三大州的市占率已達 60%，往上成
長空間有限。 TaDa Gaming 在美國發展的 Social SS 不只侷限在
三大洲，而是透過代理商在法規允許的 35 個州推廣，包括原來
的三大州，且可用手機遊玩， 2024 年開始積極推廣， 2H25 已有
一個月幾千萬元的營收貢獻， 01~08/2026 成長加速，加上基期
和市占率仍偏低，故公司樂觀表示，美國的 Social SS 會是未來
1~2 年公司的主要成長動能，美國的比重在未來會逐步提升。東
南亞授權的部分， 2H26~2027 年還是看好會持續成長，不過因
為基期已墊高，能新切入的區域及規模較小，成長會較
2023~2025 年明顯減緩。網路遊戲的部分，因為台灣同類型遊戲
眾多，競爭非常激烈， 2026 年預估營收為小幅下滑， 2027 年此
趨勢仍難以改變。商用遊戲機的部分，原本 03/2026 法說預估
2026 年營收 YOY 為小幅成長，不過因為中國景氣尚未明顯復
甦，許多遊樂場在整頓，造成許多中古機台流入市面，影響公
司新機的推廣，故修正為 2026 年營收小幅衰退。

### Page 2

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 24 日

2 群益投顧 
  ESG(Environment, Social, Governance) ：

公司簡介：  
鈊象 (3293 TT) 成立於 1989 年，製作商用電子遊戲機起家，較著名的有《黃
飛鴻》、《三國戰記》和《西遊釋厄傳》等等。隨著街機的式微， 2000 年新
增個人電腦遊戲的設計與研發，並成功開發出 《明星三缺一》 單機遊戲， 2004
年則開發連線撮合遊戲平台 Game 淘，該平台包含可連線對戰的《明星 3 缺
1 Online 》。 2007 年推出的《唯舞獨尊 Online 》亦是鈊象的主力遊戲之一。
2010 年後，智慧手機崛起加上網速的提升，帶動手遊蓬勃發展，鈊象也將
重心轉往手遊市場，推出 《金猴爺》 、 《明星三缺一手機版》 、 《滿貫大亨》 、
《金好運》、《海王寶藏》和《金虎爺》等博弈手遊。 2017 年開始經營美國
授權遊戲市場，採用授權給當地代理商的方式，收取授權金和服務費，經過
多年的經營，取得市佔第一的佳績。東南亞授權則是 2021 年開始布局，與
美國授權一樣只收取授權金和服務費，不同的地方在於：東南亞授權有很多
代理商，美國授權則有兩個代理商；東南亞授權經營了 1~1.5 年就看到成
效，美國授權則是經營了 4 年。 01~08/2026 營收比重的部分，網路遊戲佔
24% ，授權遊戲佔 74% ，商用遊戲機佔 2%。  
美國的 Social SS 因基期和市占率仍偏低，為未來 1~2 年的主要成長動能：
展望 2H26~2027 年，遊戲營收排名方面， 01~08/2026 營收排行榜為東南亞
授權、 TaDa Gaming 、 《明星三缺一》 、美國授權、 《金猴爺》 、 《金好運》 、
《滿貫大亨》、《海王寶藏》，該排名與 01~02/2026 一致。區域營收比重
方面， 01~08/2026 為東南亞 54% ，台灣 16% ，中國 5%，美國 14% ，其他
11% ，若與 01~02/2026 和 2025 年相比，台灣和中國比重下滑，美國和其他
比重提升，東南亞則持平。以實際營收來看， 01~08/2026 東南亞仍有 YOY 
+18% 的成長，美國和其他地區則是 YOY +50% 以上的成長，台灣和中國則
為 YOY 衰退。  
授權遊戲的部分，美國和其他地區營收大幅成長，主要是 TaDa Gaming 在
拓展美國和中南美市場已明顯看到成效，歐洲和非洲則因為競爭激烈，仍在
努力推廣中。其中美國市場過去是以三大州為主，為過去常講的 SS 或是美
國授權，在三大州的市占率已達 60%，往上成長空間有限。 TaDa Gaming 在

### Page 3

個股報告  
                                        2026 年 9 月 24 日

3 群益投顧 
美國發展的 Social SS 不只侷限在三大洲，而是透過代理商在法規允許的 35
個州推廣，包括原來的三大州，且可用手機遊玩， 2024 年開始積極推廣，
2H25 已有一個月幾千萬元的營收貢獻， 01~08/2026 成長加速，加上基期和
市占率仍偏低，故公司樂觀表示，美國的 Social SS 會是未來 1~2 年公司的
主要成長動能，美國的比重在未來會逐步提升。東南亞授權的部分，
2H26~2027 年還是看好會持續成長，不過因為基期已墊高，能新切入的區域
及規模較小，成長會較 2023~2025 年明顯減緩。  
網路遊戲的部分，因為台灣同類型遊戲眾多，競爭非常激烈， 2026 年預估
營收為小幅下滑， 2027 年此趨勢仍難以改變。  
商用遊戲機的部分，原本 03/2026 法說預估 2026 年營收 YOY 為小幅成長，
不過因為中國景氣尚未明顯復甦，許多遊樂場在整頓，造成許多中古機台流
入市面，影響公司新機的推廣，故修正為 2026 年營收小幅衰退。  
綜合以上，預估 2026 年營收為 264.33 億元， YOY +19.97% ；稅後純益為
134.05 億元， YOY +23.53% ；稅後 EPS 47.57 元。預估 2027 年營收為 320.76
億元， YOY +21.35% ；稅後純益為 167.54 億元， YOY +24.99% ；稅後 EPS 
59.45 元。  
投資建議：  
2027 年 TaDa Gaming 在美國發展 Social SS 已明顯看到成效，因基期和市占
率仍偏低，故 Social SS 會是未來 1~2 年營收的主要成長動能，考量 2027 年
展望正向且評價偏低，給予 Buy 的投資建議。

7 群益投顧 
  【投資評等說明】  
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首次評等潛在上漲空間 ≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy) 5% ≤首次評等潛在上漲空間 <15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無法由基本面給予投資評等  
預期近期股價將處於盤整  
建議降低持股

【投資評等調整機制】  
  投資評等係「首次」給予特定個股投資評等時，其前一日收盤價相較 3 個月
目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為四個等級，定義如上。
爾後的投資評等係依循「首次評等」，直到停止推薦。   
「強力買進」、「買進」及「區間操作」均有 upside 目標價。差別僅在於，
首次評等時潛在報酬率不同。「中立」則無目標價。  
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」或「區間操作」之投資評等，
就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基本面及
股價變化，直到停止推薦。  
停止推薦情境：  
1.  達目標價。  
2.  雖未達原訂目標價，但檢視基本面、訊息面、籌碼面等多方訊息，研
判股價上漲空間已然有限，將適時出具降評報告。  
3.  推薦後股價不漲反跌。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」 。我
們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來預測
之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投資目的
或依預定對象之財務狀況所撰寫出來的，因此，此研究報告的目的，既非對
投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提供詢價服
務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值及收益，可
能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投資來得少的
情形。

1 群益投顧 
材料 *-KY (4763 TT)  2Q26 因山東廠停工歲修，毛利率降至  40.9% ， 3Q26 已回升至近  
50%；扣 除 匯 回 美 元 3 億 資 本 之 一 次 性 稅 款 約 新 台 幣 9.6 億元後 ，稅後 EPS 0.72 元 。
2027 年併入  Cerdia 全 球 產 能 後，年 產 能 達 絲 束 16.40 萬噸與醋片  19.50 萬噸 。全
球 絲 束 市 場 由 五 大 業 者 濃 縮 為 四 大 業 者 ， 公 司 採 取 控 制 接 單 策 略 ， 預計  10 ~11 /2026
進行  2027 年 合 約 調 價。

研 究 員 ： 吳 健 鉦   
A51755@cim.capital.com.tw

材料 *-KY 法 說 會 展 望 正 面， 4Q26 將 調 整 2027 年 合 約 價 格：  
材 料 *-KY 於  09/ 21 /2026 宣布以約美元  3.68 億 收 購 全 球 醋 酸 纖 維 大 廠  
Cerdia 100% 股 權 。 交 易 架 構 由 兩 部 分 構 成， 材料 -KY 以 自 有 資 金 支 付 現
金 對 價 約 美 元 2.14 億 ， 佔 比 約  58% ； 其 餘 發 行 新 股 109,295 仟股 ， 佔比
約  42% ， 交 易 完 成 後 賣 方 黑 石 集 團 實 體 將 持 有 材 料 -KY 增 資 後 約  9.95% 
股 權 ， 集 團 股 本 由 9.89 億 股 增 至 10.98 億股。   
被 收 購 方  Cerdia 2025 年 營 收 為 美 元  7.31 億 ， 調 整 後  EBITDA 為 美 元  
2.67 億， EBITDA 利潤率為  36.5% 。 截 至  06/2026 之近  12 個 月 ， 雙 方
合 計 營 收 約 達 10 億 美 元 ， 合 併 EBITDA 達 4 億 美 元 ， 合 併 EBITDA 利
潤率為  39% 。 此 交 易 使 材 料-KY 由 原 中 國 山 東 單 一 生 產 基 地 ， 擴 增 至 德 國
弗萊堡、美國金斯波特、巴西聖安德烈及俄羅斯謝爾普霍夫等全球五大基
地 。 合 併 後 全 球 醋 酸 纖 維 絲 束 設 計 產 能 達 16.40 萬噸 /年 ， 醋 酸 纖 維 素 醋 片
設 計 產 能 達 19.50 萬噸 /年。   
財 務 結 構 改 善 為 本 次 收 購 的 核 心 獲 利 驅 動 力 。Cerdia 目 前 帳 上 存 續 美 元 9 
億 私 債，到 期 日 為 2031 年，票 面 利 率 約 9%，年 利 息 支 出 達 美 元 0.81 億。
管 理 層 規 劃 於  2027 年 交 割 並 表 後 進 行 債 務 再 融 資 ， 透 過 台 系 與 歐 系 銀 行
將 利 率 降 至 4.5% –6% ， 預 估 每 年 節 省 約 美 元 0.41 億 利 息 支 出 ， 扣 稅 後 將
直 接 挹 注 合 併 淨 利 。 
材料 *-KY 2Q26 毛 利 率 降 至 40.9% ，主 因 山 東 各 廠 進 行 3 週至  1 個 月 歲
修 ， 停 工 固 定 費 用 全 數 列 入 當 期 銷 貨 成 本 ，3Q26 毛 利 率 已 回 升 至 近 五 成 ；
3Q26 另 認 列 自 山 東 匯 回 美 元 3 億 資 本 之 10% 一 次 性 所 得 稅， 約 為 0.30 
億美元 (約 新 台 幣 9.6 億元 )， 不 影 響 本 業 長 期 獲 利 。  
產業與產品布局上，全球絲束市場由五大業者濃縮為四大業者。 公司 採取
調控供給策略，將山東部分產能轉向長絲、短絲及不織布，維持絲束市場
供給緊縮以支撐產品價格。收購宣布後引發下游避險訂單，公司採取控制
接單策略，將談判遞延至  10/2026 至  11/2026 進行  2027 年 合 約 調 價 。
產品分工方面，材料 -KY 負 責 長 絲 、 短 絲 及 塑 膠 級 醋 片 ， 切 入 紡 織 、 眼 鏡
框與  AI 穿戴材料； Cerdia 則主打  85 種濾嘴規格及  100% 生物可降解
濾 嘴。材 料 *-KY 具 備 眼 鏡 膠 框 熱 解 回 收 專 利，正 與 東 莞 大 型 眼 鏡 廠 合 作 處
理每年約  1.65 萬 噸 廢 料 ， 預 計 12/2026 正 式 公 告 。

3 群益投顧 
  【 投 資 評 等 說 明 】 
  評等  定義  
  強力買進 (Strong Buy)  首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間≥ 35%  
  買進 (Buy)  15%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<35%  
  區間操作 (Trading Buy)  5%≤ 首 次 評 等 潛 在 上 漲 空 間<15%

中立 (Neutral)  
無 法 由 基 本 面 給 予 投 資 評 等 
預 期 近 期 股 價 將 處 於 盤 整 
建 議 降 低 持 股

【 投 資 評 等 調 整 機 制 】 
  投 資 評 等 係 「 首 次 」 給 予 特 定 個 股 投 資 評 等 時 ， 其 前 一 日 收 盤 價 相 較3 個
月目標價之潛在上漲空間計算而得。個股投資評等分為 四 個等級，定義如
上 。 爾 後 的 投 資 評 等係 依 循 「 首 次 評 等 」 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。  
「 強 力 買 進 」、「 買 進 」及「 區 間 操 作 」均有 upside 目 標 價 。差 別 僅 在 於 ，
首 次 評 等 時 潛 在 報 酬 率 不 同 。 「 中 立 」 則 無 目 標 價 。 
一旦我們給予特定個股「強力買進」 、 「買進」 或「區間操作」 之投資評
等，就是責任的開始，爾後將透過各式研究報告作定期性、持續性追蹤基
本 面 及 股 價 變 化 ， 直 到 停 止 推 薦 。 
停 止 推 薦 情 境 ： 
1.  達 目 標 價 。 
2.  雖 未 達 原 訂 目 標 價 ， 但 檢 視 基 本 面 、 訊 息 面 、 籌 碼 面 等 多 方 訊 息 ，
研 判 股 價 上 漲 空 間 已 然 有 限 ， 將 適 時 出 具 降 評 報 告 。 
3.  推 薦 後 股 價 不 漲 反 跌 ， 亦 將 出 具 降 評 報 告 。

【免責聲明】  
  本研究報告僅提供予特定人之客戶作為參考資料「非經同意不得轉載」。
我們並不確保此資訊的完整性與正確性，投資人應了解，報告中有關未來
預測之陳述可能不會實現，因而不應被依賴。而且此報告並非根據特定投
資 目 的 或 依 預 定 對 象 之 財 務 狀 況 所 撰 寫 出 來 的，因 此，此 研 究 報 告 的 目 的 ，
既非對投資人於買賣證券、選擇權、期貨或其他證券相關之衍生性商品提
供詢價服務，亦非作為進行交易的要約。投資人應注意到相關證券之價值
及收益，可能會有無預警地上升或下降，產生投資回報金額可能比原始投
資 來 得 少 的 情 形 。

研 究  報 告                
中磊(5388.TT) 
產業：電子–通信網路 
三大產品線皆穩健成⻑，惟 2027 年記憶體供需仍持續緊俏 
 投資評價與建議 
看好中磊受惠10G PON、Wi-Fi 7與DOCSIS 4.0網通規格升級以及5G FWA放
量，預估2026/2027年稅後EPS為5.72/7.92元，過往中磊forward PE區間多落
在9.5~15倍，雖然今明年訂單能見度佳，惟考量1H26逐步採用高成本記憶體
生產，同時2027年記憶體供需仍緊俏，故從2027年獲利評價，給予11倍PER。  
 重點評論分析 
Wi-Fi 7規格全面導入，2Q26營收大幅增加，惟毛利率受記憶體漲價承壓：
2Q26營收季增23.9%至206.2億元，係因轉嫁記憶體漲價成本以及Wi-Fi 7規
格產品全面放量 ， 毛利率季減1.36ppts ， 係受記憶體漲價影響 ， 稅後EPS為1.58
元 。 2Q26季底存貨金額較1Q26增加約130億元 ， 其中約80億元為記憶體成品 ，
用於滿足3Q26訂單需求。隨著第三季進入網通出貨旺季，預估3Q26營收季增
9.5%至225.8億元，稅後EPS為1.52元。 
三大產品線均穩健成⻑ ， 惟2027年記憶體供需持續緊俏 ：展望2026~2027年 ，
營收動能來自 ： (1)Broadband CPE產品受惠Fiber PON產品往10G以上規格迭
代，Cable DOCSIS從3.1往4.0升級以及5G FWA出貨量增加；(2)DAA分散式
局端設備目前大型設備商客戶認證時程遞延，預估1Q27將逐步開始出貨，有
利提升後續毛利率表現；(3)Enterprise產品則受惠Wi-Fi 7滲透率提升，目前
Wi-Fi 7比重已達無線產品出貨量之70%。 
 投資風險 
記憶體等零組件漲價與缺料。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
Wi-Fi 7規格全面導入，2Q26營收大幅增加，惟毛利率受記憶體漲價承壓，進入出貨旺季，3Q26營收持續走升：
2Q26營收季增23.9%至206.2億元 ，營收成⻑中約有⼀半來自記憶體成本轉嫁客戶以及Wi-Fi 7規格產品全面放量 ，
毛利率大幅季減1.36ppts，係受記憶體漲價影響，營業費用率因營收規模擴大而季減1.71ppts至9%，營業利益率
季增0.3ppts至3.3%，稅後EPS為1.58元。資產負債表方面，2Q26季底存貨金額較1Q26增加約130億元，其中約
80億元為記憶體，主要用於滿足3Q26訂單需求。由於記憶體缺貨將造成下游電信營運商客戶無法正常出貨，進而
導致消費者轉往其他同業，將喪失市佔率，故確保「正常交貨」成為網通廠和電信營運商首要目標，公司亦持續
與客戶協商轉嫁成本。隨第三季進入網通出貨旺季，預估3Q26營收季增9.5%至225.8億元，稅後EPS為1.52元。 
三大產品線均穩健成⻑ ， 惟2027年記憶體供需持續緊俏 ：展望2026~2027年，中磊營收動能來自：(1)Broadband 
CPE產品受惠Fiber PON產品往10G以上規格迭代 ， 同時近期中磊領先於同業於SCTE TechExpo26發表50G PON ，
提供客戶下世代光纖產品，另外Cable DOCSIS逐步從3.1往4.0規格升級以及5G FWA出貨量增加；(2)DAA分散式
局端設備透過將大局端設備到⼩節點間改鋪光纖，最後⼀哩路才採用Cable，用於解決訊號衰減與延遲之問題，目
前大型設備商客戶認證時程遞延，預估1Q27將逐步開始出貨，有利提升後續毛利率表現；(3)Enterprise產品受惠
Wi-Fi 7規格滲透率持續提升，目前Wi-Fi 7比重已達無線產品出貨量之70%。綜上所述，預估2026/2027年營收年
增53.9%/14.1%至831/948.4億元，稅後EPS為5.72/7.92元。

治療藥物將取證，AB-BNCT 邁向商業化階段 
 B10 L-BPA 治療藥物 2026 年底取得台灣藥證 ：禾榮科 AB-BNCT 設
備已於 2024 年 7 月正式取得 TFDA 醫療器材許可證 ， 其核心治療新
藥 B10 L -BPA 之復發型頭頸癌已完成收案，主要評估指標為治療後
三個月的客觀反應率（ORR） ，預計2026 年底取得藥證，取得藥證
後 ， 將可正式收治病患 ， 且未來將擴大適應症至三陰性乳癌與肺腺癌 。 
 1H27 年北榮 BNCT 機台將完成驗收：禾榮科積極布局國內外市場，
在國內已與中國醫藥大學新竹附設醫院展開產學合作， 2024 年 11
月正式啟動臨床收案；臺北榮民總醫院的建置工程則於 2025 年 8 月
動工，預計 1Q27 完工，1H27 可認列新台幣 12 億元營收；海外方
面，佳世達集團旗下的南京明基醫院斥資 5500 萬美元採購設備，同
時禾榮科技攜手新加坡國立大學醫院共建研發樞紐 ， 成功將台灣自主
研發的 AB-BNCT 癌症治療技術推向國際舞台。 
 禾榮科治療藥物即將取得許可證，屆時即可正式開始收費，同時，
1H27 將認列台北榮總 BNCT 設備 12 億元營收 ，2027 年開始轉虧為
盈，未來隨著適應症擴展及海外布局，營收與獲利將爆發成長，禾榮
科投資評等維持買進，預估 2027 年 EPS 6.29 元，目標價 700 元。

個股報告 
禾 榮 科 核 心 技 術 為 自 主 研 發
的 AB -BNCT 癌症治療設備  
 禾榮科成立於 2017 年，專注於發展先進的癌症治療技術，公司核心業務為加
速器型硼中子捕獲治療（Accelerator-Based Boron Neut ron Capture Therapy, 
AB-BNCT），此項技術源自清華大學與工業技術研究院歷經三十年的研究成
果，禾榮科成功將這項學研基礎轉化為商業應用，提供完整的 AB-BNCT 整體
解決方案，涵蓋質子加速器系統、中子源靶系統、中子束調整器、治療控制系
統、治療計畫系統以及病人定位系統等關鍵設備，致力於為醫療機構與患者帶
來精準、高效的治療選擇。

B10 L -BPA 治 療 藥 物 預 計
2026 年底取得台灣藥證  
 禾榮科技 AB-BNCT 設備已於 2024 年 7 月正式取得 TFDA 醫療器材許可證，其
核心治療新藥 B10 L-BPA (10B-L-Boronophenylalanine)正進行復發型頭頸癌、
惡性腦瘤及復發型腦膜瘤的臨床試驗，復發型頭頸癌收案 24 人，主要評估指標
為治療後三個月的客觀反應率 （ORR） ，預計2026 年底取得藥證，目前禾榮科
營收仍主要來自恩慈治療，取得藥證後，將可正式收治病患，且未來將擴大適
應症至三陰性乳癌與肺腺癌等；此外，搭配術前精準評估劑量的 F18 FBPA 診
斷用藥已於 4Q25 進入臨床試驗。

禾 榮 科 獲 利 來 自 「 設 備 銷
售 、 設 備 維 保 、 治 療 藥 物 、
診斷藥物」四大支柱  
 在設備銷售方面，禾榮科技提供買斷、租賃與分潤等多元商業模式， AB-BNCT
系統單台售價約為新台幣 12 至 16 億元，設備售出後每年可穩定收取設備售價
5%至 9%的維修保養費用，若採分潤模式，BNCT 治療費用為 60 萬元，加藥費
為 100 萬元，禾榮科可抽取治療費用的 30%至 40%作為收益。

在藥物佈局上，核心治療藥物 BPA 平均每人次收費為新台幣 40 萬元，目前於
臨床試驗與恩慈治療中使用，待取得藥證後即可獨立進行商業化銷售；而診斷
藥物 FBPA 每次費用約新台幣 4 至 8 萬元，且其用量遠大於治療藥物。

## Document 269: GFHK - CPO _ NPO

Source PDF: all report pdf/GFHK - CPO _ NPO.pdf
Report source: 未公開來源
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 4
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Equity Research 
Sector Report | Networking｜Sep 29, 2026 
CPO Accelerates, Yet Stock Implications Vary

Overseas  
Technology

What’s New?  
Following our earlier notes of CPO/NPO Accelerates on Aug  17, we believe that Nvidia’s CPO scale-out switch progress is faster than 
expected. On the other hand, for NPO, due to slower than expected AWS T4 rollout, we revise our total OE shipments forecast to 6.5m/42m 
in 2027E/2028E respectively. We provide our view on recent development and key stocks below. 
Comments  
Nvidia’s Scale-out CPO:  Following Nvidia’s Aug ‑14 announcement, Spectrum ‑X CPO switches have entered mass production. To 
support the ramp, TSMC has expanded CPO testing/inspection capacity, notably adding Insertion 2/3 equipment. Key components such 
as FAU are also progressing well, helped by improving multi ‑plug/unplug yield rates. At the system level, we believe major component 
suppliers are already on site, enabling cleanroom ‑level system integration from early Sep t. We now forecast Nvidia’s CPO switch 
shipments at 15k/16k/30k/100k for 4Q26/1Q27/2Q27/2027. 
Nvidia & AWS Scale-up NPO: Per our AI Platform Value Shift report on Aug 6, NVL576‑based scale‑up CPO/NPO systems are expected 
to begin adoption in 2H27E, with two switch boards per NVSwitch tray, each board carrying four ASICs, paired with four 3.2T OEs/ASIC. 
We reiterate our view that CPO (QM5 + 4 OEs) and NPO (QM5 + 4 OEs on sockets) are progressing in parallel, and our view is similar 
with Street expectations for full NPO adoption at NVL576 launch given NPO maturity. The next to watch is Feynman, which our supply  
chain research indicate is also being developed in both CPO and NPO variants; packaging ‑side visibility suggests CPO (QM6 with six 
6.4T OEs) remains on track. For AWS, given limited near‑term visibility on T4 tape‑out, we now shift our Trainium 4 launch to 1Q28. We 
forecast AWS OE consumption at 0m/12m units for 2H27E/2028E. Incorporating ASIC and AMD volumes, we now expect global scale‑up 
OE demand to reach 6.5m/42m in 2027E/2028E.  
Recommendations   
• Likes: 1) Lumentum (LITE Buy) on CPO acceleration which supports valuation, with CPO/NPO revenue contribution of 12%/26% in 
CY27/CY28; 2) Browave (3163 TT) & Bizlink (3665 TT Buy)  on CPO’s shuffle box of ~8x content vs. NPO ’s, with production to 
accelerate in 1Q27E. 3) Semtech (SMTC Buy) on TIA/Driver for NPO, despite our downward revision, due to tight supply.  
• Dislikes: Largan (3008 TT) as our analysis indicates EPS contribution of NT$20/NT$97 in CY27/CY28, assuming shipments begin 
in 2H27, suggesting implied 20x CY28 valuation which is not particularly cheap, with further risk on market share given the incumbent 
supplier’s ongoing yield improvements and a growing number of new entrants.  
Risks   
1) AI demand slowdown, 2) supply constraint, 3) competition.
